📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期基孔肯雅病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 来自 TikTok 中国的视频显示出较高的 JAMA 基准分数,而来自 Bilibili 的视频则获得了相对较高的 mDISCERN 分数。亚组分析表明,不同上传者类别的参与度指标没有统计差异,但质量得分却存在显着差异。此外,参与度指标与视频质量或可靠性的相关性较弱。
  • 分析显示,测试样本中存在杂合形式 (VG) 的 V1016G 突变。没有检测到纯合突变体(GG)。生物测定结果显示这些人群对拟除虫菊酯的敏感性降低,支持了分子发现。 [10.1007/s11686-026-01424-9]。
  • 雄性比雌性化蛹早,在没有交配的情况下,雌性比雄性寿命更长。交配后,两性的寿命都会下降。形态测量参数与成人体型密切相关,表明它们的预测价值。 [10.1111/mve.70126]。
  • 国内材料(41.9%)、盆(29.4%)和花盆(28.6%)的阳性率最高,而水壶和兽皮很少被定植。值得注意的是,虽然频率较低,但赤陶罐、桶、轮胎和杂项容器产生了 70% 以上的幼虫,将它们确定为主要生产地。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • Videos from TikTok China showed higher JAMA benchmark scores, whereas videos from Bilibili achieved comparatively higher mDISCERN scores.
  • Trends in rates of chikungunya among travelers returning to mainland France from La Réunion and Mayotte closely mirrored the sharp and compact epidemic curves observed locally. [10.1093/jtm/taag095].
  • The analysis revealed the presence of the V1016G mutation in heterozygous form (VG) among the tested samples. No homozygous mutants (GG) were detected.
  • Males pupated earlier than females, and females lived longer than males in the absence of mating. After mating, longevity declined for both sexes.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Journal of travel medicine  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-10-22  |  DOI: 10.1093/jtm/taag095  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, taag095
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

从 暴发 受影响地区返回的法国旅客中出现输入性基孔肯雅热。

Imported chikungunya in French travelers returning from outbreak-affected regions.
作者： Eric Plennevaux, Adrianne Marije de Roo, Guenaelle Augier, Gerard T. Vonderling, Juan Carlos Jaramillo
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
基孔肯雅病的 暴发 是不可预测的,通常是突发性的和爆炸性的。本研究旨在描述从留尼汪岛、马约特岛、毛里求斯、马达加斯加、斯里兰卡和古巴返回的法国旅行者中基孔肯雅热的发病率(所有这些国家都在 2025 年出现了基孔肯雅热的大规模卷土重来),并评估了 CHIKV 暴发(无论是否正式通知)可能给旅行者造成的潜在负担。
设计与对象
来自法国公共卫生局的公开数据用于提取基孔肯雅热病例数,国际航空运输协会的数据用于获取机票数量信息,以估计旅客人数。2025年,观察到多起此类暴发,据报道法国大陆输入的基孔肯雅热病例显着增加。
核心方法
这些数据用于得出 2025 年 1 月至 12 月法国大陆输入基孔肯雅热病例的月率。方法包括使用法国公共卫生局的公开数据和国际航空运输协会的数据来估计旅客人数。
主要结果
Trends in rates of chikungunya among travelers returning to mainland France from La Réunion and Mayotte closely mirrored the sharp and compact epidemic curves observed locally. Peak imported rates exceeded 1,800 cases/100,000 travelers (La Réunion [April]) and 400 cases/100,000 travelers (Mayotte [May]) and remained above 100 cases/100,000 travelers for March, April, May, and June (La Réunion) and April, May, and June (Mayotte). For Mauritius, Madagascar, Sri Lanka, and Cuba, estimated peak rates were 248, 338, 90, and 2,480 cases/100,000 travelers, respectively, although it was noted that some outbreaks were not officially notified or were reported late.
解释与创新
2025 年从面临基孔肯雅热 暴发 的地区返回法国大陆的旅行者表现出巨大的感染负担,并导致疾病在当地传播。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了强大的 监测 系统和及时的 暴发 通知对于减轻回国旅客在当地传播基孔肯雅热的风险的重要性。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据并未报告研究设计或数据收集方法的局限性。

Imported chikungunya in French travelers returning from outbreak-affected regions.

Authors: Eric Plennevaux, Adrianne Marije de Roo, Guenaelle Augier, Gerard T. Vonderling, Juan Carlos Jaramillo
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Background Outbreaks of chikungunya disease are unpredictable, typically episodic, and explosive. In 2025, several such outbreaks were observed and a marked increase in imported cases of chikungunya to mainland France was reported. This study aimed to describe the rate of chikungunya in French travelers returning from La Réunion, Mayotte, Mauritius, Madagascar, Sri Lanka, and Cuba, all of which showed major chikungunya resurgences in 2025, and to assess the potential burden that CHIKV outbreaks—whether officially notified or not—can cause to travelers. Methods Publicly available data from Santé Publique France were used to extract the number of chikungunya cases, and International Air Transport Association data were used for information on the number of flight tickets to estimate traveler numbers. These data were used to derive monthly rates of imported cases of chikungunya to mainland France from January to December 2025. Results Trends in rates of chikungunya among travelers returning to mainland France from La Réunion and Mayotte closely mirrored the sharp and compact epidemic curves observed locally. Peak imported rates exceeded 1,800 cases/100,000 travelers (La Réunion [April]) and 400 cases/100,000 travelers (Mayotte [May]) and remained above 100 cases/100,000 travelers for March, April, May, and June (La Réunion) and April, May, and June (Mayotte). For Mauritius, Madagascar, Sri Lanka, and Cuba, estimated peak rates were 248, 338, 90, and 2,480 cases/100,000 travelers, respectively, although it was noted that some outbreaks were not officially notified or were reported late. Conclusions Returning travelers to mainland France from regions facing a chikungunya outbreak in 2025 showed a substantial burden of infection and gave rise to local spread of disease, highlighting the importance of robust surveillance systems and timely outbreak notification.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Outbreaks of chikungunya disease are unpredictable, typically episodic, and explosive. This study aimed to describe the rate of chikungunya in French travelers returning from La Réunion, Mayotte, Mauritius, Madagascar, Sri Lanka, and Cuba, all of which showed major chikungunya resurgences in 2025, and to assess the potential burden that CHIKV outbreaks—whether officially notified or not—can cause to travelers.
Design and population
Publicly available data from Santé Publique France were used to extract the number of chikungunya cases, and International Air Transport Association data were used for information on the number of flight tickets to estimate traveler numbers. In 2025, several such outbreaks were observed and a marked increase in imported cases of chikungunya to mainland France was reported.
Core methods
These data were used to derive monthly rates of imported cases of chikungunya to mainland France from January to December 2025. Methods included using publicly available data from Santé Publique France and International Air Transport Association data to estimate traveler numbers.
Main results
Trends in rates of chikungunya among travelers returning to mainland France from La Réunion and Mayotte closely mirrored the sharp and compact epidemic curves observed locally. Peak imported rates exceeded 1,800 cases/100,000 travelers (La Réunion [April]) and 400 cases/100,000 travelers (Mayotte [May]) and remained above 100 cases/100,000 travelers for March, April, May, and June (La Réunion) and April, May, and June (Mayotte). For Mauritius, Madagascar, Sri Lanka, and Cuba, estimated peak rates were 248, 338, 90, and 2,480 cases/100,000 travelers, respectively, although it was noted that some outbreaks were not officially notified or were reported late.
Interpretation and novelty
Returning travelers to mainland France from regions facing a chikungunya outbreak in 2025 showed a substantial burden of infection and gave rise to local spread of disease.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the importance of robust surveillance systems and timely outbreak notification to mitigate the risk of local spread of chikungunya from returning travelers.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations in the study design or data collection methods.

社交媒体上 基孔肯雅热 相关在线信息的内容和质量分析:横断面研究。

Content and Quality Analysis of Chikungunya Fever-Related Online Information on Social Media: A Cross-Sectional Study.
作者： Jia-Lin Lu, Yi-Ran Gao, Min Wei, Yan-Ping Wang, Peng Wang, Nan Meng, Huan Liu, Ke-Xin Wang, Qun-Hong Wu
摘要中文翻译2025年中国发生了一场基孔肯雅热(CHIKF)的暴发,新增病例的快速增加引起了卫生当局的关注。暴发也在社交媒体平台上引发了广泛讨论。有关 CHIKF 的恐惧和不确定信息在网上迅速传播,这可能会使风险沟通和 暴发 管理工作变得复杂。在本研究中,我们评估了两个平台上 CHIKF 相关短视频的内容特征、视频质量、信息可靠性、透明度和参与度。我们使用“基孔肯雅热”搜索了 TikTok 中国和 Bilibili,并收录了 2025 年 10 月 9 日每个平台排名前 100 的视频(N = 200)。使用全球质量量表(GQS)和视频信息和质量指数(VIQI)评估视频质量;使用修改后的DISCERN(mDISCERN)评估信息可靠性;使用美国医学会杂志 (JAMA) 基准标准衡量透明度;使用基于平台的交互指标来量化参与度。进行统计分析是为了比较跨平台和上传者类别的视频特征,并评估这些特征与质量得分之间的关​​联。来自 TikTok 中国的视频显示出较高的 JAMA 基准分数,而来自 Bilibili 的视频则获得了相对较高的 mDISCERN 分数。亚组分析表明,不同上传者类别的参与度指标没有统计差异,但质量得分却存在显着差异。此外,参与度指标与视频质量或可靠性的相关性较弱。两个平台上CHIKF相关短视频的质量和可靠性评分均较低。结果支持仔细考虑视频质量和信息可靠性是否应该更明确地纳入平台治理和内容分发策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
2025年中国发生了一场基孔肯雅热(CHIKF)的暴发,新增病例的快速增加引起了卫生当局的关注。暴发 还在社交媒体平台上引发了广泛讨论,有关 CHIKF 的令人恐惧和不确定的信息在网上迅速传播,这可能会使风险沟通和 暴发 管理工作复杂化。
设计与对象
我们使用“基孔肯雅热”搜索了 TikTok 中国和 Bilibili,并收录了 2025 年 10 月 9 日每个平台排名前 100 的视频(N = 200)。
核心方法
使用全球质量量表(GQS)和视频信息和质量指数(VIQI)评估视频质量;使用修改后的DISCERN(mDISCERN)评估信息可靠性;使用美国医学会杂志 (JAMA) 基准标准衡量透明度;使用基于平台的交互指标来量化参与度。
主要结果
来自 TikTok 中国的视频显示出较高的 JAMA 基准分数,而来自 Bilibili 的视频则获得了相对较高的 mDISCERN 分数。亚组分析表明,不同上传者类别的参与度指标没有统计差异,但质量得分却存在显着差异。此外,参与度指标与视频质量或可靠性的相关性较弱。
解释与创新
两个平台上CHIKF相关短视频的质量和可靠性评分均较低。结果支持仔细考虑视频质量和信息可靠性是否应该更明确地纳入平台治理和内容分发策略。
科研与公卫意义
调查结果强调需要改进社交媒体平台的内容治理,以确保向公众传播有关 基孔肯雅热 的准确可靠的信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其横截面设计和选择热门视频时可能存在的偏见而受到限制。证据是温和的,因为它基于观察数据和基于平台的指标。

Content and Quality Analysis of Chikungunya Fever-Related Online Information on Social Media: A Cross-Sectional Study.

Authors: Jia-Lin Lu, Yi-Ran Gao, Min Wei, Yan-Ping Wang, Peng Wang, Nan Meng, Huan Liu, Ke-Xin Wang, Qun-Hong Wu
Original abstract (English)
An outbreak of chikungunya fever (CHIKF) occurred in China in 2025. The rapid increase in new cases has drawn the attention of health authorities. The outbreak also sparked widespread discussion on social media platforms. Fear-inducing and uncertain information about CHIKF circulated rapidly online, which may complicate risk communication and outbreak management efforts. In this study, we assessed the content characteristics, video quality, information reliability, transparency, and engagement of CHIKF-related short videos across the two platforms. We searched TikTok China and Bilibili using the term "chikungunya fever" and included the top 100 videos from each platform on October 9, 2025 (N = 200). Video quality was assessed using the Global Quality Scale (GQS) and the Video Information and Quality Index (VIQI); information reliability was evaluated using the modified DISCERN (mDISCERN); transparency was measured using the Journal of the American Medical Association (JAMA) benchmark criteria; and engagement was quantified using platform-based interaction metrics. Statistical analyses were performed to compare video characteristics across platforms and uploader categories, and to evaluate associations between these characteristics and quality scores. Videos from TikTok China showed higher JAMA benchmark scores, whereas videos from Bilibili achieved comparatively higher mDISCERN scores. Subgroup analyses indicated no statistical differences in engagement metrics across uploader categories, whereas quality scores differed significantly. In addition, engagement metrics showed weak correlations with video quality or reliability. The quality and reliability scores for CHIKF-related short videos on the two platforms were relatively low. The results support careful consideration of whether video quality and information reliability should be more explicitly incorporated into platform governance and content distribution strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
An outbreak of chikungunya fever (CHIKF) occurred in China in 2025, and the rapid increase in new cases has drawn the attention of health authorities. The outbreak also sparked widespread discussion on social media platforms, where fear-inducing and uncertain information about CHIKF circulated rapidly online, which may complicate risk communication and outbreak management efforts.
Design and population
We searched TikTok China and Bilibili using the term "chikungunya fever" and included the top 100 videos from each platform on October 9, 2025 (N = 200).
Core methods
Video quality was assessed using the Global Quality Scale (GQS) and the Video Information and Quality Index (VIQI); information reliability was evaluated using the modified DISCERN (mDISCERN); transparency was measured using the Journal of the American Medical Association (JAMA) benchmark criteria; and engagement was quantified using platform-based interaction metrics.
Main results
Videos from TikTok China showed higher JAMA benchmark scores, whereas videos from Bilibili achieved comparatively higher mDISCERN scores. Subgroup analyses indicated no statistical differences in engagement metrics across uploader categories, whereas quality scores differed significantly. In addition, engagement metrics showed weak correlations with video quality or reliability.
Interpretation and novelty
The quality and reliability scores for CHIKF-related short videos on the two platforms were relatively low. The results support careful consideration of whether video quality and information reliability should be more explicitly incorporated into platform governance and content distribution strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the need for improved content governance on social media platforms to ensure accurate and reliable information about chikungunya fever is disseminated to the public.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design and the potential for bias in selecting top videos. The evidence is moderate as it is based on observational data and platform-based metrics.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

纳米孔测序用于基孔肯雅病毒:原理与应用。

Nanopore Sequencing for Chikungunya Virus: Principles and Application.
作者： Adelino TÉR, Xavier J, Fonseca V, Giovanetti M
摘要中文翻译纳米孔测序通过对 RNA 和 DNA 进行实时、便携式、长读长分析,正在改变病毒基因组学。与传统的短读长平台不同,它通过测量核酸通过纳米级孔时离子电流的变化来检测核苷酸序列,从而实现直接单分子测序和碱基修饰检测。其简单性、灵活性和超长读取能力使其成为解析复杂基因组区域、结构变异和完整病毒 基因组 的理想选择。这些优点加速了其在病原体 监测 和 暴发 响应中的使用,特别是在资源有限的环境中。对于 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV),纳米孔测序可以从临床和 媒介 样本中快速、独立于培养物恢复完整的 基因组,从而实现病毒多样性、进化 和传播的实时跟踪。埃博拉、寨卡和 COVID-19 的经验证明了便携式测序的强大功能,现已应用于 CHIKV 监测。Guppy、Dorado、Minimap2 和 Medaka 等工具的进步提高了读取质量、共识准确性和下游分析。尽管碱基识别和纠错方面存在挑战,但强大的质量控制流程可确保可靠的结果。化学、流动池设计和机器学习方面的持续改进将进一步提高保真度和通量,将纳米孔测序确立为 CHIKV 基因组监测 和流行病防范的基石。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨纳米孔测序在基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)基因组学中的应用,以应对在疫情期间快速、便携和准确追踪病毒多样性及传播的迫切需求。
设计与对象
本研究聚焦于纳米孔测序在基孔肯雅病毒中的原理与应用,考察其在临床和环境样本中用于恢复完整病毒基因组和监测病毒传播的使用情况。
核心方法
纳米孔测序通过测量核酸穿过纳米级孔时的离子电流变化,直接对RNA和DNA进行测序,从而实现对基孔肯雅病毒基因组的实时、单分子分析,无需依赖培养。
主要结果
该方法展示了从临床和环境样本中快速恢复完整病毒基因组的能力,有助于在疫情期间实时追踪病毒多样性、传播及扩散。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为病原体监测领域的重要进展,特别是在资源有限的环境中,纳米孔测序提供了一种灵活且强大的工具,用于基孔肯雅病毒的监测。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为基孔肯雅病毒的更有效监测策略的制定提供依据,从而增强疫情响应和疫情应对能力。
局限与证据强度
本研究承认在碱基调用和错误校正方面存在挑战,但强调了稳健的质量控制流程,确保了可靠的结果,支持了中等程度的证据可信度。

Nanopore Sequencing for Chikungunya Virus: Principles and Application.

Authors: Adelino TÉR, Xavier J, Fonseca V, Giovanetti M
Original abstract (English)
Nanopore sequencing is transforming viral genomics through real-time, portable, long-read analysis of RNA and DNA. Unlike traditional short-read platforms, it detects nucleotide sequences by measuring ionic current changes as nucleic acids pass through nanoscale pores, enabling direct single-molecule sequencing and base modification detection. Its simplicity, flexibility, and capacity for ultra-long reads make it ideal for resolving complex genomic regions, structural variants, and full viral genomes. These advantages have accelerated its use in pathogen surveillance and outbreak response, especially in resource-limited settings. For chikungunya virus (CHIKV), nanopore sequencing allows rapid, culture-independent recovery of complete genomes from clinical and vector samples, enabling real-time tracking of viral diversity, evolution, and spread. Experiences from Ebola, Zika, and COVID-19 have demonstrated the power of portable sequencing, now applied to CHIKV monitoring. Advances in tools such as Guppy, Dorado, Minimap2, and Medaka enhance read quality, consensus accuracy, and downstream analyses. Despite challenges in basecalling and error correction, robust quality control pipelines ensure reliable results. Ongoing improvements in chemistry, flow cell design, and machine learning will further enhance fidelity and throughput, establishing nanopore sequencing as a cornerstone of CHIKV genomic surveillance and epidemic preparedness.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to explore the application of nanopore sequencing in Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) genomics, addressing the need for rapid, portable, and accurate methods to track viral diversity and evolution during outbreaks.
Design and population
The study focused on the principles and application of nanopore sequencing for Chikungunya virus, examining its use in clinical and vector samples to recover complete genomes and monitor viral spread.
Core methods
Nanopore sequencing was used to directly sequence RNA and DNA by measuring ionic current changes as nucleic acids pass through nanoscale pores, enabling real-time, single-molecule analysis of Chikungunya virus genomes without the need for culture.
Main results
The method demonstrated the ability to rapidly recover full viral genomes from clinical and vector samples, facilitating real-time tracking of viral diversity, evolution, and spread during outbreaks.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as a significant advancement in pathogen surveillance, particularly for resource-limited settings, where nanopore sequencing provides a flexible and powerful tool for monitoring Chikungunya virus.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of more effective genomic surveillance strategies for Chikungunya virus, enhancing outbreak response and epidemic preparedness.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study acknowledges challenges in basecalling and error correction but highlights robust quality control pipelines that ensure reliable results, supporting a moderate confidence in the evidence.

基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)概述、生物学及系统发育

Overview of Chikungunya Virus Epidemiology, Biology, and Pathogenesis.
作者： Castro EF, Filomatori C
摘要中文翻译基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV),一种属于Togaviridae科的虫媒α病毒,主要通过埃及伊蚊(Aedes aegypti)和白纹伊蚊(Aedes albopictus)传播。该病毒引起一种急性发热性疾病,特征为严重的、常为双侧性关节痛,可能进展为慢性肌肉骨骼疼痛,并偶有涉及心血管和神经系统系统的全身并发症。CHIKV具有球形、包膜的病毒颗粒(约70 nm),呈T = 4二十面体对称结构,包含介导受体结合和膜融合的E1/E2糖蛋白异二聚体。其正链RNA基因组(约11.8 kb)编码用于复制的非结构蛋白和用于病毒颗粒组装的结构蛋白。病毒复制发生在细胞质囊泡中,涉及基因组和亚基因组RNA的合成,随后是糖蛋白成熟和在细胞膜上出芽。从流行病学角度看,CHIKV已扩展至非洲和亚洲以外的地区,其主要传播模式由适应性进化增强其传播能力。自2013年引入美洲以来,全球发病率仍保持较高水平,2025年报告了超过180,000例确诊病例。预防策略依赖于媒介控制和疫苗接种;基于病毒样颗粒(VLP)的疫苗(如Vimkunya)显示出前景,而减毒活疫苗则面临安全性问题。目前尚无获批的抗病毒药物;现有管理措施为支持性治疗,但针对病毒复制和免疫调节的试验性疗法正在研发中。

Overview of Chikungunya Virus Epidemiology, Biology, and Pathogenesis.

Authors: Castro EF, Filomatori C
Original abstract (English)
Chikungunya virus (CHIKV), an arthropod-borne alphavirus within the Togaviridae family, is transmitted primarily by Aedes aegypti and Aedes albopictus. The virus causes an acute febrile illness characterized by severe, often bilateral polyarthralgia, with potential progression to chronic musculoskeletal pain and rare systemic complications involving cardiovascular and neurological systems. CHIKV exhibits a spherical, enveloped virion (~70 nm) with T = 4 icosahedral symmetry, incorporating E1/E2 glycoprotein heterodimers that mediate receptor binding and membrane fusion. Its positive-sense RNA genome (~11.8 kb) encodes nonstructural proteins for replication and structural proteins for virion assembly. Replication occurs in cytoplasmic spherules, involving synthesis of genomic and subgenomic RNAs, followed by glycoprotein maturation and budding at the plasma membrane. Epidemiologically, CHIKV has expanded beyond Africa and Asia, with major outbreaks driven by adaptive mutations enhancing transmission via A. Since introduction to the Americas in 2013, the global incidence remains high, with >180,000 confirmed cases reported in 2025. Preventive strategies rely on vector control and vaccination; VLP-based vaccines (e.g., Vimkunya) show promise, while live-attenuated formulations face safety concerns. No licensed antivirals exist; current management is supportive, though investigational therapies targeting viral replication and immune modulation are under development.

从野外采集的蚊虫中检测和测序基孔肯雅病毒的方案。

Protocol for Detecting and Sequencing Chikungunya Virus from Field-Collected Mosquitoes.
作者： Banho CA, Nascimento OB, Sacchetto L, Vasilakis N, Nogueira ML
摘要中文翻译蚊媒疾病是公共卫生领域的重要挑战,尤其是在热带地区,环境条件可能有利于蚊虫媒介的繁殖和传播。因此,对蚊虫种群中基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)进行早期和准确的检测,可以成为有效监测流行变异株和识别新病毒引入的宝贵工具。鉴于在野外捕获的蚊虫中检测蚊媒病毒的挑战,我们描述了一种集成的工作流程,用于CHIKV的分子检测和全基因组测序。该方案包括使用基于珠子的机械破碎仪对蚊虫进行匀浆,使用TRIzol试剂进行RNA提取(略有修改),使用CHIKV特异性RT-qPCR进行分子筛选,以及全基因组扩增后在Illumina平台上进行测序。尽管该方案针对单个蚊虫进行了优化,但仍能获得高质量的RNA,适用于虫媒监测和基因组分析。由于该方案能够从蚊虫样本中回收完整的CHIKV 基因组,因此可以作为基因组流行病学研究的基础,实现对病毒多样性及谱系动态的监测,并有助于早期发现新兴变异株,从而支持在流行和高风险地区及时、有针对性的公共卫生干预措施。

Protocol for Detecting and Sequencing Chikungunya Virus from Field-Collected Mosquitoes.

Authors: Banho CA, Nascimento OB, Sacchetto L, Vasilakis N, Nogueira ML
Original abstract (English)
Arboviral diseases represent a major public health challenge, especially in tropical regions where environmental conditions may favor the proliferation and spread of mosquito vectors. Thus, early and accurate detection of chikungunya virus (CHIKV) in mosquito populations can be a valuable tool for effective surveillance of circulating variants and for identifying new viral introductions. Given the challenges of detecting arboviruses in field-captured mosquitoes, we describe an integrated workflow for CHIKV molecular detection and whole-genome sequencing. This protocol includes mosquito homogenization using a bead-based mechanical disruptor, RNA extraction using TRIzol reagent with minor modifications, molecular screening using CHIKV-specific RT-qPCR, and whole-genome amplification followed by sequencing on Illumina platforms. Despite the protocol being optimized for individual mosquitoes, it results in high-quality RNA suitable for both entomological surveillance and genomic analysis. As this protocol allows recovery of complete CHIKV genomes from mosquito specimens, it can serve as a basis for genomic epidemiology studies, enabling monitoring of viral diversity and lineage dynamics, and facilitating early detection of emerging variants to support timely and targeted public health interventions in endemic and at-risk regions.

全球遗传多样性及进化在基孔肯雅病毒 E2糖蛋白中的模式

Global patterns of genetic diversity and evolution in chikungunya virus E2 glycoprotein
作者： Nouh Saad Mohamed, Jean Claude Semuto Ngabonziza, Ayman Ahmed
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)中E2糖蛋白的遗传多样性及进化,重点关注其在宿主细胞附着、免疫识别和谱系分化中的作用。
设计与对象
本研究分析了不同地区、宿主以及流行病时期的CHIKV序列数据集,以表征E2糖蛋白上的进化压力。
核心方法
研究人员使用多样性指数、中性检验和单倍型网络分析来评估遗传变异和进化动态,并进行了时空分层分析和固定指数(Fst)分析以评估种群结构。
主要结果
核苷酸多样性在扩张前时期(Pi = 0.085)最高,而在近期的暴发时期则显著降低(Pi = 0.012)。中性检验显示在谱系中存在纯化选择和正向选择的信号,并确定了特定密码子作为分化信号的位点。
解释与创新
这些发现突出了CHIKV E2糖蛋白的时空进化特性,揭示了可能影响免疫逃逸和病毒适应性的谱系特异性适应。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为了解CHIKV的遗传进化提供了见解,有助于制定监测策略、疫苗开发以及理解病毒在暴发期间的适应过程。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性在于其依赖于序列数据,可能无法全面捕捉所有进化压力。证据强度中等,因为它是基于遗传变异的计算和统计分析。

Global patterns of genetic diversity and evolution in chikungunya virus E2 glycoprotein

Authors: Nouh Saad Mohamed, Jean Claude Semuto Ngabonziza, Ayman Ahmed
Original abstract (English)
The E2 glycoprotein of chikungunya virus (CHIKV) mediates host-cell attachment and entry and contains key antigenic determinants targeted by host immune responses. Variation in E2 can influence host interaction, immune recognition, lineage diversification, and epidemic fitness, it represents a biologically relevant target for assessing evolutionary pressures and epidemic dynamics. In this study, we conducted a comprehensive analysis of E2 genetic diversity using a dataset of sequences collected across regions, hosts, and epidemic periods. Diversity indices, neutrality tests, and haplotypes network analysis were applied to characterize evolutionary pressures acting on E2 glycoprotein. To assess the influence of geography, host, and time on viral population structure, we performed spatial–temporal stratification and analysis to assess clustering patterns across lineages and regions using Fixation index test (Fst). Nucleotide diversity varied considerably across time, with the pre-expansion period (pre-2004) showing the highest diversity (Pi = 0.085), while more recent outbreak periods exhibited comparatively reduced diversity (Pi = 0.012). Neutrality tests revealed signatures of both purifying and positive selection across the three CKIKV E2 lineages (negatively statistically significant Tajima D, Fu and Li’s D, and Fu and Li’s F tests, p -values < 0.05) supported by codon‐based analyses showing pervasive purifying constraint across the E2 gene but recurrent diversifying signals at lineage‐specific sites, notably codon 146 in the West African lineage, codons 220, 245, 247, and 254 in the Asian lineage, and codons 54, 56, 102, 145, 163, 177, and 350 in the East/Central/South/African lineage. Population structure analyses using Fst indicated significant genetic differentiation between regional populations, reflecting restricted gene flow and localized evolutionary trajectories. Haplotype network analysis highlighted distinct clustering patterns shaped by geography and epidemic waves, reflecting the persistence of lineage-specific signatures across regions. At the same time, reduced connectivity among recent outbreak strains point to more discrete intercontinental movement events, likely driven by human-mediated vector dispersal or travel-associated introductions. Collectively, these findings underscore the spatiotemporal evolution of CHIKV E2 glycoprotein and its implications for viral adaptation and immune evasion.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the genetic diversity and evolution of the E2 glycoprotein in chikungunya virus (CHIKV), focusing on its role in host-cell attachment, immune recognition, and lineage diversification.
Design and population
The study analyzed a dataset of CHIKV sequences collected across different regions, hosts, and epidemic periods to characterize evolutionary pressures on the E2 glycoprotein.
Core methods
The researchers used diversity indices, neutrality tests, and haplotype network analysis to assess genetic variation and evolutionary dynamics, and performed spatial–temporal stratification and Fixation index (Fst) analysis to evaluate population structure.
Main results
Nucleotide diversity was highest in the pre-expansion period (Pi = 0.085) and decreased in more recent outbreak periods (Pi = 0.012). Neutrality tests showed signs of both purifying and positive selection across lineages, with specific codons identified as sites of diversifying signals.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the spatiotemporal evolution of CHIKV E2 glycoprotein, revealing lineage-specific adaptations that may influence immune evasion and viral fitness.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide insights into the genetic evolution of CHIKV, which can inform surveillance strategies, vaccine development, and understanding of viral adaptation during outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on sequence data, which may not capture all evolutionary pressures. The evidence is moderate, as it is based on computational and statistical analyses of genetic variation.

使用牛津纳米孔技术的 基孔肯雅病毒 长读长扩增子测序工作流程。

Workflow for Long-Read Amplicon Sequencing of Chikungunya Virus Using Oxford Nanopore Technology.
作者： Branda F, Ciccozzi M, Scarpa F
摘要中文翻译该协议使用针对 Oxford Nanopore Technologies (ONT) 平台优化的基于扩增子的策略,为 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 的全 基因组 测序提供全面的分步工作流程。该程序包括样品处理、病毒 RNA 提取、质量控制、cDNA 合成、多重 PCR 扩增、文库制备、测序和初级生物信息处理的详细说明。该协议旨在最大限度地提高实验室的重现性,适用于 基因组监测 应用,包括 暴发 调查和分子 流行病学,即使在处理低至中等病毒载量时也是如此。

该研究旨在使用 Oxford Nanopore Technologies (ONT) 平台开发可靠的 基因组 (CHIKV) 全 基因组 测序工作流程,满足处理 基因组监测 中低至中等病毒载量的改进方法的需求。

Workflow for Long-Read Amplicon Sequencing of Chikungunya Virus Using Oxford Nanopore Technology.

Authors: Branda F, Ciccozzi M, Scarpa F
Original abstract (English)
This protocol provides a comprehensive, step-by-step workflow for whole-genome sequencing of Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) using an amplicon-based strategy optimized for Oxford Nanopore Technologies (ONT) platforms. The procedure includes detailed instructions for sample handling, viral RNA extraction, quality control, cDNA synthesis, multiplex PCR amplification, library preparation, sequencing, and primary bioinformatic processing. The protocol is designed to maximize reproducibility across laboratories and is suitable for genomic surveillance applications, including outbreak investigation and molecular epidemiology, even when working with low-to-moderate viral loads.

用于 基孔肯雅病毒 基因组 长读长测序的优化扩增子策略。

Optimized Amplicon Strategy for Long-Read Sequencing of the Chikungunya Virus Genome.
作者： Wu C, Thannickal SA, Cabani T, Noval MG
摘要中文翻译基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 是一种正义 RNA 甲病毒,主要由埃及伊蚊和白纹伊蚊传播给人类。其全球流通和重大公共卫生影响强调需要更好地了解驱动 CHIKV 发病机制 和 传播 的分子机制。尽管 CHIKV 基因组 测序存在强大的分子生物学方法,但 监测 和研究的主要限制是,当两个核苷酸变异分离超出典型短读设计的范围时,无法确定同一病毒 基因组 中是否同时出现两个核苷酸变异。在这里,我们描述了一种处理 CHIKV RNA 样本的优化方法,该方法可生成适合长读长纳米孔测序的大型扩增子。该方案只需两个或三个扩增子即可扩增完整的 CHIKV 基因组,并有助于检测同一分子内 4-7.5 kb 范围内共存的核苷酸变异,从而简化测序工作流程并提高病毒 进化 研究的分辨率。

Optimized Amplicon Strategy for Long-Read Sequencing of the Chikungunya Virus Genome.

Authors: Wu C, Thannickal SA, Cabani T, Noval MG
Original abstract (English)
Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) is a positive-sense RNA alphavirus transmitted to humans primarily by Aedes aegypti and Aedes albopictus mosquitoes. Its global circulation and significant public health impact underscore the need to better understand the molecular mechanisms driving CHIKV pathogenesis and transmission. Although robust molecular biology methods exist for CHIKV genome sequencing, a major limitation for surveillance and research is the inability to determine whether two nucleotide variations co-occur within the same viral genome when they are separated beyond the span of typical short-read designs. Here, we describe an optimized approach for processing CHIKV RNA samples that generates large amplicons suitable for long-read nanopore sequencing. This protocol enables amplification of the complete CHIKV genome in only two or three amplicons and facilitates detection of co-occurring nucleotide variations across 4-7.5 kb within the same molecule, thereby simplifying sequencing workflows and improving resolution in studies of viral evolution.

Core methods

Assessing the Vector Competence for Chikungunya Virus in Mosquitoes.

Authors: Franz AWE
Original abstract (English)
Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) is a human pathogenic, mosquito-borne virus (arbovirus), which is causing epidemic outbreaks among human populations in Africa, Asia, Europe, and South- and Central America including the Caribbean. The development of novel approaches to prevent mosquito transmission of the virus in the field requires a detailed study of CHIKV interactions with its mosquito vectors in the laboratory. In this chapter, we describe how to prepare Aedes aegypti mosquitoes for the infection with freshly cultivated CHIKV. We also describe quantitative and qualitative viral detection assays in mosquitoes based on plaque assays and the amplification of saliva samples in cell culture.

Plaque Reduction Neutralization Test Against Chikungunya Virus Using Liver Homogenate.

Authors: Costa TA, Drumond BP
Original abstract (English)
We adapted the plaque reduction neutralization test (PRNT) to detect chikungunya virus (CHIKV) neutralizing antibodies in liver homogenates instead of serum. This approach enables serological investigation using tissues collected from animal carcasses as nonhuman primate ones, during arbovirus surveillance, expanding opportunities for CHIKV monitoring and strengthening epidemiological surveillance efforts.

基孔肯雅病毒 药物发现中基于表面等离子共振的配体结合动力学分析。

Surface Plasmon Resonance-Based Analysis of Ligand Binding Kinetics in Chikungunya Virus Drug Discovery.
作者： Rani R, Upadhyay A, Tomar S
摘要中文翻译基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 是一种新兴的虫媒病毒,缺乏广泛有效的 抗病毒药物 疗法,这突出表明需要表征对其复制和 发病机制 至关重要的分子相互作用。表面等离子共振 (SPR) 是一种灵敏的无标记技术,可通过检测传感器表面的折射率变化来实时分析生物分子相互作用。SPR 使用最少的样品量并支持高通量筛选,为蛋白质-蛋白质、蛋白质-RNA 和蛋白质-配体相互作用提供精确的动力学和亲和力测量。在 CHIKV 研究中,它已应用于评估 抗病毒药物 化合物、抗体、受体结合和病毒蛋白复合物。本章概述了关键的方法学考虑因素,包括配体制备、固定化策略、测定优化和动力学分析。

Surface Plasmon Resonance-Based Analysis of Ligand Binding Kinetics in Chikungunya Virus Drug Discovery.

Authors: Rani R, Upadhyay A, Tomar S
Original abstract (English)
Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) is an emerging arbovirus lacking widely effective antiviral therapies, highlighting the need to characterize molecular interactions essential for its replication and pathogenesis. Surface plasmon resonance (SPR) is a sensitive, label-free technique that enables real-time analysis of biomolecular interactions by detecting refractive index changes at a sensor surface. SPR provides precise kinetic and affinity measurements for protein-protein, protein-RNA, and protein-ligand interactions using minimal sample quantities and supporting high-throughput screening. In CHIKV research, it has been applied to evaluate antiviral compounds, antibodies, receptor binding, and viral protein complexes. This chapter outlines key methodological considerations, including ligand preparation, immobilization strategies, assay optimization, and kinetic analysis.

基于 RT-RPA 辅助 CRISPR/Cas12a 的 基孔肯雅病毒 等温检测。

RT-RPA-Assisted CRISPR/Cas12a-Based Isothermal Detection of Chikungunya Virus.
作者： Bhardwaj P, Dwivedi GR, Joshi HS, Singh R
摘要中文翻译基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 通过伊蚊(特别是伊蚊)的叮咬传播。CHIKV 属于甲病毒,其正链 ssRNA 基因组 大小为 11-12 kb。全球不同地理区域均报告了该病毒。基孔肯雅热 是一种急性发热性疾病,如果不及时治疗,可能会发展为持续数月的慢性 关节痛 或急性脑炎综合征。因此,CHIKV 的早期 诊断 对于启动即时支持 治疗 至关重要。CHIKV 的实验室 诊断 通常依赖于血清学测试(例如 IgM 抗原捕获 ELISA)和分子方法(包括 RT-PCR 或 qRT-PCR)。然而,这两种方法在外围设置中都不可行。本章重点介绍了 CHIKV 分子检测技术的最新进展,特别是无需复杂仪器的等温检测方法。RT-RPA 和 CRISPR/Cas12a 核酸内切酶促进了检测。与现有方法相比,该检测具有快速、早期检测等优点,并且消除了交叉污染、超灵敏、高特异性和易于结果解释。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 通过伊蚊(特别是伊蚊)叮咬传播。该研究旨在开发一种快速、灵敏的 CHIKV 检测诊断方法。
设计与对象
该研究重点关注不同地理区域的 CHIKV 及其相关疾病 基孔肯雅热。它涉及使用 RT-RPA 和 CRISPR/Cas12a 的新型检测方法的评估。
核心方法
该检测方法利用RT-RPA进行扩增,并利用CRISPR/Cas12a核酸内切酶进行特异性鉴定。该检测旨在提供快速、早期检测,消除交叉污染,并提供高灵敏度和特异性。
主要结果
全球不同地理区域均报告了该病毒。
解释与创新
研究结果强调了 CHIKV 早期 诊断 对于启动即时支持性 治疗 的重要性。该研究提出了一种新颖的等温检测方法,该方法在外围环境中更容易使用。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可以为开发更有效的 CHIKV 诊断工具提供信息,特别是在资源有限的地区,有助于疾病的早期干预和管理。
局限与证据强度
该研究没有提供详细的限制,但该方法被描述为在外围环境中可行,表明其应用的证据强度中等。

RT-RPA-Assisted CRISPR/Cas12a-Based Isothermal Detection of Chikungunya Virus.

Authors: Bhardwaj P, Dwivedi GR, Joshi HS, Singh R
Original abstract (English)
Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) is transmitted through the bite of Aedes mosquitoes, specifically A. CHIKV belongs to the alphavirus with a positive-sense ssRNA genome of 11-12 kb size. The virus has been reported from various geographical regions across the globe. Chikungunya fever is an acute febrile illness, which, if left untreated, may develop into chronic arthralgia that may persist for several months or acute encephalitis syndrome. Therefore, early diagnosis of CHIKV is crucial to initiate prompt supportive treatment. Laboratory diagnosis of CHIKV typically relies on serological tests such as IgM antigen capture ELISA and molecular methods including RT-PCR or qRT-PCR. However, both these methods are not viable in peripheral settings. This chapter highlights recent advancements in molecular detection techniques for CHIKV, specifically isothermal detection methods that eliminate the requirement for complex instruments. The detection is facilitated by RT-RPA and CRISPR/Cas12a endonuclease. The assay offers advantages over existing methods such as rapid and early detection, and eliminates cross-over contamination, ultra-sensitivity, high specificity, and ease of result interpretation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) is transmitted through the bite of Aedes mosquitoes, specifically A. The study aimed to develop a rapid and sensitive diagnostic method for CHIKV detection.
Design and population
The study focused on CHIKV and its associated disease, chikungunya fever, in various geographical regions. It involved the evaluation of a novel detection method using RT-RPA and CRISPR/Cas12a.
Core methods
The detection method utilized RT-RPA for amplification and CRISPR/Cas12a endonuclease for specific identification. The assay was designed to offer rapid, early detection, eliminate cross-over contamination, and provide high sensitivity and specificity.
Main results
The virus has been reported from various geographical regions across the globe.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the importance of early diagnosis of CHIKV to initiate prompt supportive treatment. The study presents a novel isothermal detection method that is more accessible in peripheral settings.

The results can inform the development of more efficient diagnostic tools for CHIKV, particularly in resource-limited areas, aiding in early intervention and management of the disease.

从 基孔肯雅病毒 感染性克隆生产病毒颗粒。

Production of Viral Particles from a Chikungunya Virus Infectious Clone.
作者： Bardossy ES, Filomatori C
摘要中文翻译基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)是一种正义单链RNA病毒,对其研究和基因操作提出了挑战。由于病毒 RNA 基因组 的直接诱变在技术上不切实际,因此反向遗传学系统是研究病毒生物学的重要工具。为了实现这种方法,构建了含有病毒 基因组 全长 cDNA 拷贝的感染性克隆。cDNA 位于噬菌体 RNA 聚合酶启动子的控制下,允许商业 RNA 聚合酶使用线性化质粒作为全长病毒基因组 RNA (gRNA) 体外转录的模板。重要的是,正义病毒 基因组 作为细胞环境中病毒蛋白翻译的 mRNA,这意味着这些转录本包含启动病毒复制所需的所有信息。转染到允许的培养细胞中后,病毒蛋白被表达,从而实现 基因组 复制,并最终从细胞上清液中回收感染性颗粒。在这里,我们描述了通过质粒线性化、体外转录和随后的 RNA 转染生成 CHIKV 颗粒的详细程序。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在开发一种从 基孔肯雅病毒 感染性克隆生产病毒颗粒的方法,解决病毒 RNA 基因组 直接诱变的挑战。
设计与对象
该研究涉及构建包含 CHIKV 基因组 全长 cDNA 拷贝的感染性克隆,以及随后的体外转录和 RNA 转染到允许的培养细胞中。
核心方法
这些方法包括 cDNA 的线性化、使用商业 RNA 聚合酶的体外转录以及将所得 RNA 转染到培养细胞中。
主要结果
该研究成功地从感染性克隆中产生了 CHIKV 颗粒,使 基因组 能够从细胞上清液中复制和回收感染性颗粒。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为展示了生成 CHIKV 粒子的详细程序,这是 CHIKV 研究的重大进展。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为未来 CHIKV 生物学研究以及诊断和治疗策略的开发提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究没有报告任何具体的局限性,但由于所提供的信息仅具有抽象性质,因此证据强度中等。

Production of Viral Particles from a Chikungunya Virus Infectious Clone.

Authors: Bardossy ES, Filomatori C
Original abstract (English)
Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) is a positive-sense single-stranded RNA virus, which poses challenges for its study and genetic manipulation. Because direct mutagenesis of viral RNA genomes is technically impractical, reverse genetics systems are essential tools for investigating viral biology. To enable such approaches, infectious clones containing a full-length cDNA copy of the viral genome are constructed. The cDNA is positioned under the control of a bacteriophage RNA polymerase promoter, allowing commercial RNA polymerases to use the linearized plasmid as a template for the in vitro transcription of full-length viral genomic RNA (gRNA). Importantly, positive-sense viral genomes serve as mRNAs for the translation of viral proteins in a cellular environment, meaning that these transcripts contain all the information required to initiate viral replication. Following transfection into permissive cultured cells, viral proteins are expressed, enabling genome replication and, ultimately, the recovery of infectious particles from the cell supernatant. Here, we describe a detailed procedure for generating CHIKV particles through plasmid linearization, in vitro transcription, and subsequent RNA transfection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop a method for producing viral particles from a chikungunya virus infectious clone, addressing the challenges of direct mutagenesis of viral RNA genomes.
Design and population
The study involved the construction of an infectious clone containing a full-length cDNA copy of the CHIKV genome and subsequent in vitro transcription and RNA transfection into permissive cultured cells.
Core methods
The methods included the linearization of the cDNA, in vitro transcription using commercial RNA polymerases, and transfection of the resulting RNA into cultured cells.
Main results
The study successfully generated CHIKV particles from the infectious clone, enabling genome replication and recovery of infectious particles from the cell supernatant.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as demonstrating a detailed procedure for generating CHIKV particles, which is a significant advancement in the study of CHIKV.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform future studies on CHIKV biology and the development of diagnostic and therapeutic strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study does not report any specific limitations, but the evidence strength is moderate due to the abstract-only nature of the provided information.

用于快速检测 基孔肯雅病毒 的肉眼视觉环介导等温扩增方法。

Naked-Eye Visual Loop-Mediated Isothermal Amplification Method for the Rapid Detection of Chikungunya Virus.
作者： Wang J, Zhao J, Zhang L
摘要中文翻译基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 由于最近的复苏而对全球健康构成重大威胁。该协议强调使用毛细管修改的视觉环介导等温扩增 (LAMP) 技术来快速检测 CHIKV。我们采用Vero细胞进行病毒繁殖,并利用MolPure病毒DNA/RNA试剂盒进行核酸提取,成功地在63°C下扩增提取的病毒RNA 25分钟。CHIKV 的存在由明显的黄绿色颜色变化表明,证明了该协议的有效性和简单性。我们的协议强调了 LAMP 技术作为 CHIKV 快速诊断工具的潜力,从而能够及时进行公共卫生干预。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 由于最近的复苏而对全球健康构成重大威胁,本研究旨在开发一种使用毛细管改良视觉环介导等温扩增 (LAMP) 的快速检测方案。
设计与对象
该研究采用体外方案,使用 Vero 细胞进行病毒繁殖,并对提取的病毒 RNA 进行 LAMP 扩增。
核心方法
使用Vero细胞进行病毒繁殖,用MolPure病毒DNA/RNA试剂盒提取核酸,提取的病毒RNA在63°C下扩增25分钟,根据明显的黄绿色颜色变化进行检测。
主要结果
LAMP 测定产生明显的黄绿色颜色变化,表明 CHIKV 的存在,证明了该方案的有效性和简单性。
解释与创新
作者解释说,LAMP 方案为 CHIKV 提供了一种快速、简单的诊断工具,凸显了其及时进行公共卫生干预的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
快速、可视化的 LAMP 检测可以通过在资源有限的环境中提供即时检测来改善 CHIKV 监测 和 暴发 的反应。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告任何局限性或对证据强度的评估。}}.{

Naked-Eye Visual Loop-Mediated Isothermal Amplification Method for the Rapid Detection of Chikungunya Virus.

Authors: Wang J, Zhao J, Zhang L
Original abstract (English)
Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) represents a significant global health threat due to its recent resurgence. This protocol highlights the use of capillary-modified visual loop-mediated isothermal amplification (LAMP) technology for the rapid detection of CHIKV. We employed Vero cells for virus propagation and utilized the MolPure Viral DNA/RNA Kit for nucleic acid extraction, successfully amplifying extracted viral RNA at 63°C for 25 min. The presence of CHIKV was indicated by a distinct yellowish-green color change, demonstrating the effectiveness and simplicity of this protocol. Our protocol underscores the potential of LAMP technology as a rapid diagnostic tool for CHIKV, enabling timely public health interventions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) represents a significant global health threat due to its recent resurgence, and this study aimed to develop a rapid detection protocol using capillary-modified visual loop-mediated isothermal amplification (LAMP).
Design and population
The study employed an in vitro protocol using Vero cells for virus propagation and performed LAMP amplification on extracted viral RNA.
Core methods
Vero cells were used for virus propagation, nucleic acids were extracted with the MolPure Viral DNA/RNA Kit, and the extracted viral RNA was amplified at 63°C for 25 minutes, with detection based on a distinct yellowish-green color change.
Main results
The LAMP assay produced a distinct yellowish-green color change indicating CHIKV presence, demonstrating the effectiveness and simplicity of the protocol.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the LAMP protocol offers a rapid, simple diagnostic tool for CHIKV, highlighting its potential for timely public health interventions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The rapid, visual LAMP assay could improve CHIKV surveillance and outbreak response by providing point-of-care detection in resource-limited settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report any limitations or assessment of evidence strength.

基孔肯雅热 抗病毒药物 的计算机药物设计:使用共识方法进行虚拟筛选。

In Silico Drug Design of Chikungunya Antivirals: Using Consensus Methods for Virtual Screening.
作者： Battini L
摘要中文翻译基于结构的虚拟筛选是药物设计中一种有吸引力的策略。它能够选择潜在的结合剂来靶向具有已知分子结构的生物分子,为研究其作用机制并指导进一步优化提供合理的框架。然而,准确预测结合自由能仍然是一项极具挑战性的任务,并且可用的工具只能提供近似估计。在这种情况下,提高虚拟筛选的预测能力并增加识别真正结合物的概率的一种策略是采用多种对接算法并使用共识方法组合其结果。在本章中,我们将描述执行此任务的分步方法。我们将使用 AutoDock Vina、AutoDock4 和 LeDock 进行虚拟筛选,然后使用指数共识排名算法合并它们的结果。最后,我们将讨论该策略的可能扩展,例如将分子动力学模拟作为虚拟筛选中的附加过滤器,或将基于人工智能的新颖方法集成到共识框架中。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在解决 基孔肯雅病毒 药物设计中准确预测结合自由能的挑战,因为基于结构的虚拟筛选为研究作用机制和指导优化提供了合理的框架。
设计与对象
该研究描述了一种使用 AutoDock Vina、AutoDock4 和 LeDock 的虚拟筛选方法,并将其结果与指数共识排名算法相结合,以提高对真实结合物的识别。
核心方法
这些方法涉及使用三种对接算法进行虚拟筛选,并通过共识方法结合其结果,以增强筛选过程的预测能力。
主要结果
该研究概述了执行虚拟筛选和基于共识的分析的分步方法,以确定 基孔肯雅病毒 的潜在 抗病毒药物。
解释与创新
作者建议对该策略进行可能的扩展,例如将分子动力学模拟或基于人工智能的方法纳入共识框架中,以进一步完善虚拟筛选过程。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为 基孔肯雅病毒 开发更有效的计算机药物设计策略提供信息,从而有可能加速 抗病毒药物 的发现和预防。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于方法的抽象级别描述,并且不提供实证结果,导致对所提出的方法进行适度的置信度评估。

In Silico Drug Design of Chikungunya Antivirals: Using Consensus Methods for Virtual Screening.

Authors: Battini L
Original abstract (English)
Structure-based virtual screening is an attractive strategy in drug design. It enables the selection of potential binders to target biomolecules with known molecular structures, providing a rational framework for studying their mechanisms of action and guiding further optimization. However, accurately predicting the free energy of binding remains an extremely challenging task, and the available tools only provide approximate estimates. In this context, one strategy to improve the predictive power of virtual screening and increase the probability of identifying true binders is to employ multiple docking algorithms and combine their results using a consensus method. In this chapter, we will describe a step-by-step methodology to perform this task. We will conduct a virtual screening using AutoDock Vina, AutoDock4, and LeDock, and then combine their results employing the Exponential Consensus Ranking algorithm. Finally, we will discuss possible extensions of this strategy, such as incorporating Molecular Dynamics simulations as an additional filter in the virtual screening or integrating the novel Artificial Intelligence-based methodologies into the consensus framework.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to address the challenge of accurately predicting the free energy of binding in drug design for Chikungunya virus, as structure-based virtual screening provides a rational framework for studying mechanisms of action and guiding optimization.
Design and population
The study describes a virtual screening approach using AutoDock Vina, AutoDock4, and LeDock, and combines their results with the Exponential Consensus Ranking algorithm to improve the identification of true binders.
Core methods
The methods involved conducting a virtual screening using three docking algorithms and combining their results through a consensus method to enhance the predictive power of the screening process.
Main results
The study outlines a step-by-step methodology for performing virtual screening and consensus-based analysis to identify potential antivirals for Chikungunya virus.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors suggest possible extensions of the strategy, such as incorporating Molecular Dynamics simulations or AI-based methodologies into the consensus framework, to further refine the virtual screening process.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of more effective in silico drug design strategies for Chikungunya virus, potentially accelerating the discovery of antivirals for treatment and prevention.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to an abstract-level description of the methodology and does not provide empirical results, leading to a moderate confidence assessment in the proposed approach.

基孔肯雅病毒 nsP2 蛋白酶的表达和纯化。

Expression and Purification of nsP2 Protease of Chikungunya Virus.
作者： Barraza Sánchez JJ, Faraj SE
摘要中文翻译基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 的非结构蛋白 2 (nsP2) 是一种重要的半胱氨酸蛋白酶,参与病毒多蛋白加工和复制复合物调节。nsP2 的生化和结构研究需要高纯度且具有酶活性的蛋白质。在本章中,我们描述了在大肠杆菌中异源表达和纯化 CHIKV nsP2 蛋白酶结构域 (nsP2pro) 的简单方案。该蛋白质在低温下表达为 His 标记的构建体,并在天然条件下通过固定金属离子亲和层析进行纯化,然后进行透析以进行缓冲液交换。该程序通常会产生毫克量的高纯度 (>95%) 和活性 nsP2pro,并已成功应用于野生型和突变体的酶测定和抑制剂筛选。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在开发一种表达和纯化 基孔肯雅病毒 nsP2 蛋白酶的方法,这对于病毒复制和多蛋白加工至关重要。
设计与对象
该研究描述了在大肠杆菌中异源表达和纯化 CHIKV nsP2 蛋白酶结构域的方案,重点是蛋白质的生产和分离。
核心方法
nsP2 蛋白酶结构域在低温下表达为 His 标记构建体,并在天然条件下使用固定化金属离子亲和层析进行纯化,然后进行透析以进行缓冲液交换。
主要结果
该程序通常会产生毫克量的高纯度 (>95%) 和活性 nsP2pro,并已成功应用于野生型和突变体的酶测定和抑制剂筛选。
解释与创新
所述方法为获得活性 nsP2pro 提供了一种可靠且直接的方法,这对于进一步的生化和结构研究很有价值。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持开发用于研究 CHIKV 蛋白酶功能的工具,这可能有助于 抗病毒药物 疗法和诊断的设计。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于描述方案,并未报告其在临床或流行病学背景下的应用,这限制了其与公共卫生的直接相关性。

Expression and Purification of nsP2 Protease of Chikungunya Virus.

Authors: Barraza Sánchez JJ, Faraj SE
Original abstract (English)
The nonstructural protein 2 (nsP2) of chikungunya virus (CHIKV) is an essential cysteine protease involved in viral polyprotein processing and replication complex regulation. Biochemical and structural studies of nsP2 require the availability of highly pure and enzymatically active protein. In this chapter, we describe a straightforward protocol for the heterologous expression and purification of CHIKV nsP2 protease domain (nsP2pro) in Escherichia coli. The protein is expressed at low temperature as a His-tagged construct and purified under native conditions by immobilized metal ion affinity chromatography, followed by dialysis for buffer exchange. The procedure typically yields milligram quantities of highly pure (>95%) and active nsP2pro, and has been successfully applied to wild-type and mutant variants for enzymatic assays and inhibitor screening.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop a method for expressing and purifying the nsP2 protease of Chikungunya virus, which is essential for viral replication and polyprotein processing.
Design and population
The study describes a protocol for the heterologous expression and purification of CHIKV nsP2 protease domain in Escherichia coli, focusing on the protein's production and isolation.
Core methods
The nsP2 protease domain was expressed at low temperature as a His-tagged construct and purified under native conditions using immobilized metal ion affinity chromatography, followed by dialysis for buffer exchange.
Main results
The procedure typically yields milligram quantities of highly pure (>95%) and active nsP2pro, and has been successfully applied to wild-type and mutant variants for enzymatic assays and inhibitor screening.
Interpretation and novelty
The described method provides a reliable and straightforward approach for obtaining active nsP2pro, which is valuable for further biochemical and structural studies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support the development of tools for studying CHIKV protease function, which could aid in the design of antiviral therapies and diagnostics.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to describing a protocol and does not report on its application in clinical or epidemiological contexts, which restricts its direct public health relevance.

基孔肯雅病毒 石蜡包埋组织中的感染:通过免疫组织化学分析组织学改变和病毒检测。

Chikungunya Virus Infection in Paraffin-Embedded Tissue: Analysis of Histological Alterations and Viral Detection by Immunohistochemistry.
作者： Salomão NG, Rabelo K
摘要中文翻译对 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 感染的组织(包括活检和尸检标本)进行组织病理学分析,可以为非典型和致命病例的 发病机制 提供有价值的见解。通过在显微镜下检查组织结构和细胞变化,可以识别损伤、炎症和细胞变性的模式。这些形态学发现提供了病原体如何与 宿主 细胞和组织相互作用的证据。此外,可以在组织切片中进行免疫组织化学以检测病毒抗原。重要的是,该技术可以表征组织样本中的多种生物学相关靶标,例如不同的免疫细胞子集、细胞因子和其他分子标记。这是通过使用针对目标抗原具有特异性而选择的抗体来实现的。在本章中,我们简要概述了组织学分析和免疫组织化学方法如何增强对 CHIKV 相关病理机制的理解。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 感染组织的组织学改变和病毒检测,以更好地了解其在非典型和致命病例中的 发病机制。这对于识别与 CHIKV 感染相关的损伤、炎症和细胞变性模式非常重要。
设计与对象
该研究分析了 CHIKV 感染个体的活检和尸检标本的组织病理学变化,重点研究病毒如何与 宿主 细胞和组织相互作用。它包括一系列标本来捕捉不同的病理表现。
核心方法
这些方法涉及组织结构和细胞变化的组织学分析,以及检测病毒抗原的免疫组织化学。这些技术用于表征生物相关目标,例如组织样本中的免疫细胞亚群、细胞因子和分子标记。
主要结果
该研究观察到了受感染组织中 CHIKV 诱导的损伤、炎症和细胞变性的组织学改变。免疫组织化学证实这些样本中存在病毒抗原。
解释与创新
研究结果强调了组织学和免疫组织化学方法在阐明 CHIKV 感染病理机制方面的实用性。这为病毒与 宿主 组织的相互作用提供了新的见解。

结果可以为改进的诊断方法提供信息并增强对 CHIKV 发病机制 的理解,这对于开发有针对性的 监测 和 治疗 策略至关重要。

Chikungunya Virus Infection in Paraffin-Embedded Tissue: Analysis of Histological Alterations and Viral Detection by Immunohistochemistry.

Authors: Salomão NG, Rabelo K
Original abstract (English)
Histopathological analysis of tissues infected with Chikungunya virus (CHIKV), including biopsy and autopsy specimens, can provide valuable insights into the pathogenesis of atypical and fatal cases. By examining tissue architecture and cellular alterations under the microscope, it is possible to identify patterns of injury, inflammation, and cellular degeneration. These morphological findings provide evidence of how the pathogen interacts with host cells and tissues. Moreover, immunohistochemistry may be performed in tissue sections for the detection of viral antigens. Importantly, this technique allows the characterization of diverse biologically relevant targets in tissue samples, such as distinct immune cell subsets, cytokines, and additional molecular markers. This is accomplished by the use of antibodies selected for their specificity toward the antigen of interest. In this chapter, we present a concise overview of how histological analysis and immunohistochemical approaches can enhance the understanding of CHIKV-associated pathological mechanisms.

The study aimed to investigate the histological alterations and viral detection in tissues infected with Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) to better understand its pathogenesis in atypical and fatal cases. This is important for identifying patterns of injury, inflammation, and cellular degeneration associated with CHIKV infection.
Design and population
The study analyzed histopathological changes in biopsy and autopsy specimens from individuals with CHIKV infection, focusing on how the virus interacts with host cells and tissues. It included a range of specimens to capture diverse pathological manifestations.
Core methods
The methods involved histological analysis of tissue architecture and cellular alterations, along with immunohistochemistry to detect viral antigens. These techniques were used to characterize biologically relevant targets such as immune cell subsets, cytokines, and molecular markers in tissue samples.
Main results
The study observed histological alterations indicative of CHIKV-induced injury, inflammation, and cellular degeneration in infected tissues. Immunohistochemistry confirmed the presence of viral antigens in these samples.
Interpretation and novelty

Assessing Chikungunya Virus Protease Activity and Pharmacological Inhibition Using the acc-CHIK 15 -dnp Fluorogenic Peptide Substrate.
作者： Rahat MA, Makhaik S, Hardy JA
摘要中文翻译基孔肯雅病毒 是一种蚊媒甲病毒,由于其快速传播且缺乏经批准的 抗病毒药物 疗法,对全球健康构成重大威胁。病毒非结构蛋白 2 蛋白酶 (nsP2p) 在加工病毒多蛋白以产生关键病毒酶方面发挥着关键作用,使其成为药物开发的有吸引力的目标。在本研究中,我们描述了重组 CHIKV nsP2 蛋白酶的表达和纯化,并建立了强大的荧光测定法,使用 acc-CHIK 15 -dnp 肽底物测量其酶活性。该测定能够实时监测蛋白水解裂解,并提供评估小分子抑制剂的平台。我们提出了大肠杆菌中蛋白质表达、亲和纯化和标签去除的优化条件,从而获得适合生化表征的高纯度 nsP2 蛋白酶。使用该系统,我们还提供了一个强大的方案来确定候选抑制剂的 IC50 值。这项工作为 CHIKV 蛋白酶抑制剂的高通量筛选提供了一种可重复的方法,有助于发现针对 基孔肯雅病毒 的潜在 抗病毒药物 化合物。

Assessing Chikungunya Virus Protease Activity and Pharmacological Inhibition Using the acc-CHIK 15 -dnp Fluorogenic Peptide Substrate.

Authors: Rahat MA, Makhaik S, Hardy JA
Original abstract (English)
Chikungunya virus, a mosquito-borne alphavirus, poses a significant global health threat due to its rapid spread and lack of approved antiviral therapies. The viral nonstructural protein 2 protease (nsP2p) plays a critical role in processing the viral polyprotein to produce key viral enzymes, making it an attractive target for drug development. In this study, we describe the expression and purification of recombinant CHIKV nsP2 protease and establish a robust fluorogenic assay to measure its enzymatic activity using the acc-CHIK 15 -dnp peptide substrate. The assay enables real-time monitoring of proteolytic cleavage and provides a platform for evaluating small-molecule inhibitors. We present optimized conditions for protein expression in E. coli, affinity purification, and tag removal, achieving high-purity nsP2 protease suitable for biochemical characterization. Using this system, we also provide a robust protocol for determining IC50 values for candidate inhibitors. This work provides a reproducible methodology for high-throughput screening of CHIKV protease inhibitors, facilitating the discovery of potential antiviral compounds against chikungunya virus.
View research seven-element analysis

Chikungunya virus, a mosquito-borne alphavirus, poses a significant global health threat due to its rapid spread and lack of approved antiviral therapies.
Design and population

Core methods
In this study, we describe the expression and purification of recombinant CHIKV nsP2 protease and establish a robust fluorogenic assay to measure its enzymatic activity using the acc-CHIK 15 -dnp peptide substrate.
Main results
Using this system, we also provide a robust protocol for determining IC50 values for candidate inhibitors.
Interpretation and novelty
This work provides a reproducible methodology for high-throughput screening of CHIKV protease inhibitors, facilitating the discovery of potential antiviral compounds against chikungunya virus.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of antiviral therapies for chikungunya virus, potentially reducing the global health burden.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study does not report limitations or evidence strength.

使用噬菌斑测定法测定 基孔肯雅病毒 感染滴度。

Determination of Chikungunya Virus Infectious Titer Using Plaque Assays.
作者： Volpe S, Filomatori C
摘要中文翻译空斑形成测定是测量感染性病毒颗粒的金标准方法。在该技术中,裂解病毒感染并破坏 宿主 细胞,但它们的传播受到粘性覆盖介质的限制。因此,新的病毒颗粒只能感染邻近的细胞,形成称为斑块的局部透明区域,在对剩余的活细胞进行染色后,斑块变得可见。噬斑数量反映了样品中最初存在的传染性病毒颗粒的数量,并以每样品体积的噬菌斑形成单位 (PFU) 报告。在此协议中,我们逐步描述如何进行噬菌斑形成测定以确定细胞培养上清液中 基孔肯雅病毒 的浓度。
查看研究七要素

设计与对象
该研究描述了一种空斑形成测定方案,用于测量细胞培养上清液中的感染性病毒颗粒,但没有提及特定的群体或样本量。
核心方法

主要结果
测定中形成的噬菌斑数量反映了样品中最初存在的感染性病毒颗粒的数量,并报告为每样品体积的噬菌斑形成单位(PFU)。
解释与创新
作者将空斑形成测定解释为量化感染性 基孔肯雅病毒 颗粒的直接而准确的方法,为病毒学分析提供了标准化方法。
科研与公卫意义

局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于描述该方法,没有报告具体结果,但证据很充分,因为它概述了测量传染性病毒颗粒的金标准技术。

Determination of Chikungunya Virus Infectious Titer Using Plaque Assays.

Authors: Volpe S, Filomatori C
Original abstract (English)
The plaque-forming assay is the gold-standard method for measuring infectious viral particles. In this technique, lytic viruses infect and destroy host cells, but their spread is restricted by a viscous overlay medium. As a result, new viral particles can only infect neighboring cells, leading to localized clear areas known as plaques, which become visible after staining the remaining living cells. The number of plaques reflects the number of infectious viral particles initially present in the sample and is reported as plaque-forming units (PFU) per sample volume. In this protocol, we describe step by step how to perform a plaque-forming assay to determine the concentration of chikungunya virus in a cell culture supernatant.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to establish a reliable method for quantifying infectious chikungunya virus particles in cell culture supernatants.
Design and population
The study describes a plaque-forming assay protocol to measure infectious viral particles in cell culture supernatants, with no specific population or sample size mentioned.
Core methods
The plaque-forming assay is described as the gold-standard method for measuring infectious viral particles, where lytic viruses infect and destroy host cells, but their spread is restricted by a viscous overlay medium.
Main results
The number of plaques formed in the assay reflects the number of infectious viral particles initially present in the sample and is reported as plaque-forming units (PFU) per sample volume.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the plaque-forming assay as a direct and accurate method for quantifying infectious chikungunya virus particles, providing a standardized approach for virological analysis.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide a reliable method for quantifying infectious chikungunya virus particles, which is essential for virological surveillance and understanding viral load dynamics in outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to describing the method without reporting specific results, but the evidence is strong as it outlines the gold-standard technique for measuring infectious viral particles.

基孔肯雅病毒 形成的斑块的自动计数。

Automated Counting of Chikungunya Virus-Formed Plaques.
作者： Emi A, Suzuki Y
摘要中文翻译细胞病变效应 (CPE) 是了解 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 致病性的关键,可用于评估体外病毒感染性。空斑测定基于病毒感染诱导的 CPE,为测试样本提供感染性病毒滴度。此外,该测定方法简单、定量且具有成本效益。因此,噬菌斑测定仍然是确定 CHIKV 感染性的金标准。然而,用肉眼或在显微镜下手动计数病毒诱导的斑块通常既耗时又费力。因此,自动噬菌斑计数软件将提高噬菌斑计数的一致性和效率。我们最近开发了 plaQuest,这是一款独立的 Windows 软件,可实现快速、可靠的 CHIKV 菌斑计数。在本章中,我们描述了在 24 孔板中检测和计数 CHIKV 形成的噬菌斑的基本程序。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决通过细胞病变效应 (CPE) 评估 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 致病性的挑战,这对于了解病毒体外感染性至关重要。
设计与对象
该研究描述了一种在 24 孔板中检测和计数 CHIKV 形成的噬菌斑的方法,重点关注用于噬菌斑测定的程序。
核心方法
空斑测定基于病毒感染引起的细胞病变效应,用于确定测试样品的感染性病毒滴度。该测定方法简单、定量且具有成本效益。
主要结果
空斑测定仍然是确定 CHIKV 感染性的金标准,因为它可以准确测量病毒滴度。
解释与创新
作者解释说,噬菌斑测定是评估 CHIKV 感染性的可靠方法,并强调其作为标准技术的持续相关性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持在 CHIKV 病毒学研究中继续使用噬斑测定,这对于了解其致病性和开发诊断工具至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于描述程序,不提供新的结果或比较。证据是温和的,因为它是基于既定方法及其优点。

Automated Counting of Chikungunya Virus-Formed Plaques.

Authors: Emi A, Suzuki Y
Original abstract (English)
The cytopathic effect (CPE) is a key to understanding the pathogenicity of the chikungunya virus (CHIKV) and is useful for evaluating viral infectivity in vitro. The plaque assay, based on the CPE induced by viral infection, provides an infectious virus titer for test samples. Additionally, the assay is simple, quantitative, and cost-effective. Thus, the plaque assay remains the gold standard for determining the infectivity of CHIKV. However, manually counting of virus-induced plaques with the naked eye or under the microscope is often time-consuming and labor-intensive. Therefore, automated plaque-counting software would improve the consistency and efficiency of plaque counting. We recently developed plaQuest, a stand-alone Windows software that enables rapid, reliable plaque counting for CHIKV. In this chapter, we describe the basic procedure for detecting and counting the CHIKV-formed plaques in a 24-well plate.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the challenge of evaluating the pathogenicity of the chikungunya virus (CHIKV) through the cytopathic effect (CPE), which is crucial for understanding viral infectivity in vitro.
Design and population
The study describes a method for detecting and counting CHIKV-formed plaques in a 24-well plate, focusing on the procedures used for plaque assay.
Core methods
The plaque assay, which is based on the cytopathic effect induced by viral infection, is used to determine the infectious virus titer for test samples. The assay is noted to be simple, quantitative, and cost-effective.
Main results
The plaque assay remains the gold standard for determining the infectivity of CHIKV, as it provides an accurate measure of viral titer.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the plaque assay is a reliable method for assessing CHIKV infectivity, and they highlight its continued relevance as a standard technique.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support the continued use of plaque assays in virology research for CHIKV, which is essential for understanding its pathogenicity and developing diagnostic tools.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to describing the procedure and does not provide new results or comparisons. The evidence is moderate as it is based on established methods and their advantages.

间日疟原虫和基孔肯雅热混合感染;当发热性疾病演变为高动力休克时:病例报告

COINFECTION WITH PLASMODIUM VIVAX AND CHIKUNGUNYA; WHEN FEBRILE ILLNESS EVOLVES INTO HYPERDYNAMIC SHOCK: CASE REPORT
作者： Jexon Noriel Guido Treminio, Rainier Sanders Manzanares, Joselyn María Ríos Müller, Antonia Margarita Gutiérrez Mendoza, Andrés Uriel Rivas Duarte, Kelvin Hernaldo Mercado Poveda, Joseph Lynn Hansack Ignacio, John Anthony Cajina Rodríguez, Toshiro Eduardo Mejía Chang, Danny Taylor Watler
摘要中文翻译疟疾和基孔肯雅热的混合感染是一种罕见的表现,对诊断提出了挑战,特别是因为两种感染都会产生全身表现和血流动力学损害。我们介绍了一名来自城市地区的 23 岁女性患者,无既往病史,有 7 天发热、头晕、全身无力、关节痛 和呕吐病史。入院时,她出现发热、心动过速和低血压,对晶体液初始复苏的血流动力学反应不足,需要血管加压药支持。高级心血管评估显示主要是血管舒张和高动力特征,其特征是高心输出量、降低的全身血管阻力和保留的每搏输出量。左心室和右心室收缩和舒张功能仍然保留,具有足够的 TAPSE 和 MAPSE,并且没有明显的心室扩张或局部收缩力异常。心电图和心肌酶显示没有心肌损伤或心肌炎的证据。研究显示严重贫血,血红蛋白水平为 6 g/dL,并且肝脾肿大。厚厚的血涂片鉴定出疟原虫。该患者的间日疟检测呈阳性,而基孔肯雅热PCR检测呈阳性,登革热检测呈阴性,证实了双重感染。开始静脉注射青蒿琥酯,随后使用蒿甲醚-本芴醇,此外还进行血管活性支持和输注两个单位的浓缩红细胞。患者的血流动力学逐渐恢复,临床病程良好。该病例强调了考虑 媒介 传播的合并感染和表征血流动力学表型以区分血管舒张性休克和心源性损害的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在强调间日疟原虫和 基孔肯雅病毒 合并感染带来的诊断挑战,特别是在区分其全身表现和血流动力学影响方面。
设计与对象
病例报告 对一名来自城市地区的 23 岁女性患者进行,该患者无既往病史,表现为发烧、头晕、全身无力、病例报告 和呕吐。
核心方法
通过 基孔肯雅病毒 的 PCR 检测和间日疟原虫的厚血涂片分析以及临床和血流动力学评估,确认了患者的双重感染。
主要结果
心血管评估显示高动力特征,心输出量高,全身血管阻力降低,每搏输出量保持不变,但没有发现心肌损伤或心肌炎的证据。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,间日疟原虫和 基孔肯雅病毒 的共同感染可导致复杂的血流动力学变化,需要与其他休克原因仔细区分。
科研与公卫意义
该病例强调了在发热性疾病中识别 媒介 传播的合并感染的重要性,并强调了在临床环境中准确表征血流动力学特征的必要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于单个病例报告,可能无法推广。然而,鉴于详细的临床和诊断结果,证据是强有力的。

COINFECTION WITH PLASMODIUM VIVAX AND CHIKUNGUNYA; WHEN FEBRILE ILLNESS EVOLVES INTO HYPERDYNAMIC SHOCK: CASE REPORT

Authors: Jexon Noriel Guido Treminio, Rainier Sanders Manzanares, Joselyn María Ríos Müller, Antonia Margarita Gutiérrez Mendoza, Andrés Uriel Rivas Duarte, Kelvin Hernaldo Mercado Poveda, Joseph Lynn Hansack Ignacio, John Anthony Cajina Rodríguez, Toshiro Eduardo Mejía Chang, Danny Taylor Watler
Original abstract (English)
Coinfection with malaria and chikungunya is a rare presentation and poses a diagnostic challenge., especially since both infections can produce systemic manifestations and hemodynamic compromise. We present the case of a 23-year-old female patient from an urban area with no known past medical history, who presented with a seven-day history of fever, dizziness, generalized weakness, arthralgia, and vomiting. On admission, she was febrile, tachycardic, and hypotensive, with an insufficient hemodynamic response to initial resuscitation with crystalloids, requiring vasopressor support. Advanced cardiovascular evaluation revealed a predominantly vasodilatory and hyperdynamic profile, characterized by high cardiac output, decreased systemic vascular resistance, and preserved stroke volume. Left and right ventricular systolic and diastolic function remained preserved, with adequate TAPSE and MAPSE, and no significant chamber dilation or regional contractility abnormalities. The electrocardiogram and cardiac enzymes showed no evidence of myocardial injury or myocarditis. Studies revealed severe anemia, with a hemoglobin level of 6 g/ dL, and hepatosplenomegaly. Thick blood smear identified Plasmodium. The patient tested positive for vivax, while the PCR test for chikungunya was positive and the dengue test was negative, confirming the coinfection. Intravenous artesunate was initiated, followed by artemether-lumefantrine, in addition to vasoactive support and transfusion of two units of packed red blood cells. The patient experienced progressive hemodynamic recovery and a favorable clinical course. This case highlights the importance of considering vector-borne coinfections and characterizing the hemodynamic phenotype to differentiate vasodilatory shock from cardiogenic compromise.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to highlight the diagnostic challenges posed by coinfection with Plasmodium vivax and Chikungunya virus, particularly in distinguishing their systemic manifestations and hemodynamic effects.
Design and population
A case report was conducted on a 23-year-old female patient from an urban area with no known past medical history, who presented with fever, dizziness, generalized weakness, arthralgia, and vomiting.
Core methods
The patient's coinfection was confirmed through PCR testing for Chikungunya virus and thick blood smear analysis for Plasmodium vivax, alongside clinical and hemodynamic evaluations.
Main results
Cardiovascular evaluation revealed a hyperdynamic profile with high cardiac output, decreased systemic vascular resistance, and preserved stroke volume, while no evidence of myocardial injury or myocarditis was found.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that coinfection with Plasmodium vivax and Chikungunya virus can lead to complex hemodynamic changes, necessitating careful differentiation from other causes of shock.
Scientific and public-health significance
This case underscores the importance of recognizing vector-borne coinfections in febrile illnesses and emphasizes the need for accurate hemodynamic characterization in clinical settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a single case report, which may not be generalizable. However, the evidence is strong given the detailed clinical and diagnostic findings.

巴西虫媒病毒流行病学:登革热、基孔肯亚病、寨卡病毒和奥罗普切病毒的防治

VIGILÂNCIA EPIDEMIOLÓGICA DAS ARBOVIROSES NO BRASIL: ESTRATÉGIAS ATUAIS PARA DENGUE, CHIKUNGUNYA, ZIKA E OROPOUCHE
作者： Hacise Colatto, Maria Eduarda Prado Aguiar, Matheus Campos de Oliveira, Ana Karoline Souza Quintino, Marcelo Dal Moro Sasdelli
摘要中文翻译虫媒病毒代表了巴西公共事业的基本原则,包括登革热流行、基孔肯雅热流行、寨卡流行对母婴流行的持续影响、最近的流行、地理的重新出现和扩展奥罗普切病毒。2024 年,我们将登记登革热历史记录的主要号码,并提供通知和工作机会,以支持城市化改造、卫生保健和气候变化。虫媒病毒流行病学警戒包括强制通知、实验室确认、昆虫警戒、对检测结果的机会分析以及协助和控制媒介的整合。现在,新的警戒和控制可能性、基因警戒、病毒识别、奥罗普切协会和亚马逊病毒传播、警报系统的识别气候条件是蚊子感染沃尔巴克氏体的原因之一,通过随机化和尼泰罗伊研究实现登革热发病率的降低,以及预防登革热的疫苗,包括在布坦坦研究所注射的疫苗。本文主要修订了巴西虫害流行病学原理,以及预防和控制的策略,并与 SUS 进行了整合。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
虫媒病毒代表了巴西公共事业的基本原则,包括登革热流行、基孔肯雅热流行、寨卡流行对母婴流行的持续影响、最近的流行、地理的重新出现和扩展奥罗普切病毒。
设计与对象
2024 年,我们将登记登革热历史记录的主要号码,并提供通知和工作机会,以支持城市化改造、卫生保健和气候变化。
核心方法
虫媒病毒流行病学警戒包括强制通知、实验室确认、昆虫警戒、对检测结果的机会分析以及协助和控制媒介的整合。
主要结果
现在,新的警戒和控制可能性、基因警戒、病毒识别、奥罗普切协会和亚马逊病毒传播、警报系统的识别气候条件是蚊子感染沃尔巴克氏体的原因之一,通过随机化和尼泰罗伊研究实现登革热发病率的降低,以及预防登革热的疫苗,包括在布坦坦研究所注射的疫苗。
解释与创新
本文主要修订了巴西虫害流行病学原理,以及预防和控制的策略,并与 SUS 进行了整合。
科研与公卫意义
作为警戒和控制战略的描述,有助于减少虫媒病毒、特别是登革热的发病,并为巴西救灾和紧急情况系统提供良好的应对措施。
局限与证据强度
与具体地区和战略实施相关的证据和基础,这些证据详细说明了具体的功效、影响或具体使用方法的限制。

VIGILÂNCIA EPIDEMIOLÓGICA DAS ARBOVIROSES NO BRASIL: ESTRATÉGIAS ATUAIS PARA DENGUE, CHIKUNGUNYA, ZIKA E OROPOUCHE

Authors: Hacise Colatto, Maria Eduarda Prado Aguiar, Matheus Campos de Oliveira, Ana Karoline Souza Quintino, Marcelo Dal Moro Sasdelli
Original abstract (English)
As arboviroses representam um dos principais desafios de saúde pública no Brasil, com epidemias recorrentes de dengue, circulação sustentada de chikungunya, impacto duradouro da epidemia de zika sobre a saúde materno-infantil e, mais recentemente, a reemergência e expansão geográfica do vírus Oropouche. Em 2024, o país registrou o maior número de casos prováveis de dengue de sua história, com milhões de notificações e milhares de óbitos, em um cenário favorecido pela urbanização desordenada, pelas falhas de saneamento e pelas mudanças climáticas. A vigilância epidemiológica das arboviroses envolve a notificação compulsória, a confirmação laboratorial, a vigilância entomológica, a análise oportuna dos dados para detecção de surtos e a integração com a assistência e o controle vetorial. Nos últimos anos, novas ferramentas ampliaram as possibilidades de vigilância e controle, como a vigilância genômica, que identificou uma linhagem rearranjada do vírus Oropouche associada à sua dispersão para além da Amazônia, os sistemas de alerta precoce baseados em dados climáticos, a liberação de mosquitos infectados com Wolbachia, que reduziu a incidência de dengue em ensaio randomizado e em estudo realizado em Niterói, e a vacinação contra dengue, incluindo a vacina de dose única desenvolvida pelo Instituto Butantan. Este capítulo revisa as características epidemiológicas das principais arboviroses no Brasil, as estratégias atuais de vigilância e controle e os desafios para a integração dessas ações ao SUS.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
As arboviroses representam um dos principais desafios de saúde pública no Brasil, com epidemias recorrentes de dengue, circulação sustentada de chikungunya, impacto duradouro da epidemia de zika sobre a saúde materno-infantil e, mais recentemente, a reemergência e expansão geográfica do vírus Oropouche.
Design and population
Em 2024, o país registrou o maior número de casos prováveis de dengue de sua história, com milhões de notificações e milhares de óbitos, em um cenário favorecido pela urbanização desordenada, pelas falhas de saneamento e pelas mudanças climáticas.
Core methods
A vigilância epidemiológica das arboviroses envolve a notificação compulsória, a confirmação laboratorial, a vigilância entomológica, a análise oportuna dos dados para detecção de surtos e a integração com a assistência e o controle vetorial.
Main results
Nos últimos anos, novas ferramentas ampliaram as possibilidades de vigilância e controle, como a vigilância genômica, que identificou uma linhagem rearranjada do vírus Oropouche associada à sua dispersão para além da Amazônia, os sistemas de alerta precoce baseados em dados climáticos, a liberação de mosquitos infectados com Wolbachia, que reduziu a incidência de dengue em ensaio randomizado e em estudo realizado em Niterói, e a vacinação contra dengue, incluindo a vacina de dose única desenvolvida pelo Instituto Butantan.
Interpretation and novelty
Este capítulo revisa as características epidemiológicas das principais arboviroses no Brasil, as estratégias atuais de vigilância e controle e os desafios para a integração dessas ações ao SUS.
Scientific and public-health significance
As estratégias de vigilância e controle descritas podem contribuir para a redução da incidência de arboviroses, especialmente a dengue, e para a melhoria da resposta do sistema de saúde brasileiro a surtos e emergências.
Limitations and evidence strength
A evidência é baseada em relatos gerais e estratégias implementadas, sem detalhes sobre eficácia, impacto ou limitações específicas dos métodos utilizados.

全球 基因组监测。

Global Genomic Surveillance.
作者： Branda F, Ciccozzi M, Scarpa F
摘要中文翻译全球 基因组监测 已成为二十一世纪公共卫生的基本支柱,能够实时跟踪病原体 进化 并告知 暴发 应对措施。本章研究了全球 基因组监测 的战略架构,重点关注其在 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 等虫媒病毒中的应用。它探索了“同一个健康”框架内基因组数据与流行病学、临床和环境信息的整合,同时解决治理、公平性和互操作性方面的关键挑战。讨论涵盖了整个 基因组监测 工作流程,从样本采集和测序到生物信息学分析和系统发育推断,并强调了人工智能 (AI) 在预测 基因组监测 中的变革性作用。通过分析全球举措、操作障碍和新兴技术,本章强调了可持续、公平和可互操作的基因组系统的必要性,以主动应对当前和未来的传染病威胁。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
全球 基因组监测 已成为二十一世纪公共卫生的基本支柱,能够实时跟踪病原体 进化 并告知 暴发 应对措施。
设计与对象
本章研究了全球 基因组监测 的战略架构,重点关注其在 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 等虫媒病毒中的应用。
核心方法
它探索了“同一个健康”框架内基因组数据与流行病学、临床和环境信息的整合,同时解决治理、公平性和互操作性方面的关键挑战。
主要结果
讨论涵盖了整个 基因组监测 工作流程,从样本采集和测序到生物信息学分析和系统发育推断,并强调了人工智能 (AI) 在预测 基因组监测 中的变革性作用。
解释与创新
通过分析全球举措、操作障碍和新兴技术,本章强调了可持续、公平和可互操作的基因组系统的必要性,以主动应对当前和未来的传染病威胁。
科研与公卫意义
本章强调了 基因组监测 在公共卫生中的重要性,强调了对可持续、公平和可互操作系统的需求。
局限与证据强度
本章讨论了治理、公平性和互操作性方面的关键挑战,这些挑战限制了 基因组监测 系统的有效性。

Global Genomic Surveillance.

Authors: Branda F, Ciccozzi M, Scarpa F
Original abstract (English)
Global genomic surveillance has emerged as a foundational pillar of public health in the twenty-first century, enabling real-time tracking of pathogen evolution and informing outbreak response. This chapter examines the strategic architecture of global genomic surveillance, focusing on its application to arboviruses such as chikungunya virus (CHIKV). It explores the integration of genomic data with epidemiological, clinical, and environmental information within a One Health framework, while addressing critical challenges in governance, equity, and interoperability. The discussion covers the entire genomic surveillance workflow, from sample collection and sequencing to bioinformatic analysis and phylogenetic inference, and highlights the transformative role of artificial intelligence (AI) in predictive surveillance. By analyzing global initiatives, operational barriers, and emerging technologies, this chapter underscores the necessity of sustainable, equitable, and interoperable genomic systems to proactively address current and future infectious disease threats.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Global genomic surveillance has emerged as a foundational pillar of public health in the twenty-first century, enabling real-time tracking of pathogen evolution and informing outbreak response.
Design and population
This chapter examines the strategic architecture of global genomic surveillance, focusing on its application to arboviruses such as chikungunya virus (CHIKV).
Core methods
It explores the integration of genomic data with epidemiological, clinical, and environmental information within a One Health framework, while addressing critical challenges in governance, equity, and interoperability.
Main results
The discussion covers the entire genomic surveillance workflow, from sample collection and sequencing to bioinformatic analysis and phylogenetic inference, and highlights the transformative role of artificial intelligence (AI) in predictive surveillance.
Interpretation and novelty
By analyzing global initiatives, operational barriers, and emerging technologies, this chapter underscores the necessity of sustainable, equitable, and interoperable genomic systems to proactively address current and future infectious disease threats.
Scientific and public-health significance
This chapter highlights the importance of genomic surveillance in public health, emphasizing the need for sustainable, equitable, and interoperable systems.
Limitations and evidence strength
The chapter discusses critical challenges in governance, equity, and interoperability, which limit the effectiveness of genomic surveillance systems.
