C 补充信息学术文献 · 研究C supplementaryAcademic literature · Research

来自茄科植物的螺甾烷皂苷抑制登革病毒复制和病毒蛋白表达,并对多种虫媒病毒具有活性。

Solanaceae-Derived Spirostane Saponins Inhibit Dengue Virus Replication and Viral Protein Expression and Exhibit Activity Against Multiple Arboviruses.
作者： Erika Vanessa Jimenez-Posada, Oscar Marino Mosquera-Martínez, Sara M Robledo, Diego Durango, Wiston Quiñones, Samuel Vizcaíno-Páez, Marlen Martinez-Gutierrez
摘要中文翻译由蚊子传播的虫媒病毒,包括登革病毒(DENV)、寨卡病毒(ZIKV)和基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV),构成了日益增长的全球健康负担,但目前针对这些感染的特定抗病毒药物疗法尚不普及。这种未满足的需求凸显了从生物相关来源(如植物来源的天然产物)中识别新型抗病毒药物化合物的重要性。本研究采用生物活性引导的方法,对八种茄科植物的甲醇提取物进行筛选,以鉴定具有抗病毒药物活性的化合物,针对Vero细胞中的DENV-2。五种提取物显著降低了病毒感染性,其中Solanum ovalifolium和Cestrum sp.在非细胞毒性浓度下实现了完全抑制。随后的分级和纯化过程分离出四种螺甾烷型甾体皂苷,其结构通过UPLC-DAD-MS和1D/2D NMR光谱法进行了表征。功能实验表明,这些化合物抑制了DENV的复制,表现为病毒RNA水平的显著降低、NS1和NS3蛋白表达的减少以及传染性病毒滴度的下降。阶段特异性分析显示,其抗病毒药物活性主要与病毒生命周期的细胞内阶段相关,这与抑制病毒复制和/或蛋白加工一致。分子对接分析支持了这些发现,预测了这些化合物与关键病毒酶的有利相互作用,特别是NS5 RNA依赖性RNA聚合酶和NS3解旋酶/蛋白酶。重要的是,分离出的化合物对ZIKV和CHIKV也表现出抗病毒药物活性,表明其对多种虫媒病毒具有交叉疗效。综上所述,这些发现将螺甾烷皂苷识别为有前景的抗病毒药物骨架,并支持其进一步开发为广谱抗病毒药物药物。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在通过探索植物来源的新化合物,解决针对蚊媒虫媒病毒(包括基孔肯雅病毒)缺乏特异性抗病毒药物的问题。
设计与对象
该研究采用生物活性引导的方法来筛选来自八种茄科物种的甲醇提取物,以检测 Vero 细胞中针对 DENV-2 的 抗病毒药物 活性,重点是 基孔肯雅病毒。
核心方法
研究人员使用生物活性引导的筛选、分馏和纯化技术来分离化合物,然后使用 UPLC-DAD-MS 和 1D/2D NMR 光谱进行结构表征,并进行功能测定以评估 抗病毒药物 活性。
主要结果
五种提取物显着降低了病毒感染性,其中包括 Solanum ovalifolium 和 Cestrum sp。在非细胞毒性浓度下实现完全抑制。分离出四种螺罗坦型甾体皂苷,并证明了 抗病毒药物 对抗 DENV-2 的活性。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,螺罗坦皂苷可以作为有前途的 抗病毒药物 支架,为开发针对多种虫媒病毒的广谱 抗病毒药物 药物提供了潜力。
科研与公卫意义
该结果为进一步研究植物源化合物作为 基孔肯雅病毒 和其他相关虫媒病毒的治疗选择奠定了基础,可能有助于 监测 和 治疗 策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体外研究结果,不涉及体内疗效或临床转化,这将证据的强度降至中等。

Solanaceae-Derived Spirostane Saponins Inhibit Dengue Virus Replication and Viral Protein Expression and Exhibit Activity Against Multiple Arboviruses.

Authors: Erika Vanessa Jimenez-Posada, Oscar Marino Mosquera-Martínez, Sara M Robledo, Diego Durango, Wiston Quiñones, Samuel Vizcaíno-Páez, Marlen Martinez-Gutierrez
Original abstract (English)
Mosquito-borne arboviruses, including dengue virus (DENV), Zika virus (ZIKV), and chikungunya virus (CHIKV), represent a growing global health burden, yet no specific antiviral therapies are currently available for most of these infections. This unmet need highlights the importance of identifying novel antiviral compounds from biologically relevant sources such as plant-derived natural products. In this study, a bioactivity-guided approach was applied to methanolic extracts from eight Solanaceae species to identify compounds with antiviral activity against DENV-2 in Vero cells. Five extracts significantly reduced viral infectivity, with Solanum ovalifolium and Cestrum sp. achieving complete inhibition at non-cytotoxic concentrations. Subsequent fractionation and purification led to the isolation of four spirostane-type steroidal saponins, structurally characterized by UPLC-DAD-MS and 1D/2D NMR spectroscopy. Functional assays demonstrated that these compounds inhibit DENV replication, as evidenced by marked reductions in viral RNA levels, NS1 and NS3 protein expression, and infectious viral titers. Stage-specific analyses revealed that their antiviral activity is primarily associated with intracellular phases of the viral life cycle, consistent with inhibition of viral replication and/or protein processing. Molecular docking analyses supported these findings by predicting favorable interactions with key viral enzymes, particularly NS5 RNA-dependent RNA polymerase and NS3 helicase/protease. Importantly, the isolated compounds retained antiviral activity against ZIKV and CHIKV, indicating cross-arboviral efficacy. Together, these findings identify spirostane saponins as promising antiviral scaffolds and support their further development as broad-spectrum antiviral agents.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the lack of specific antiviral therapies for mosquito-borne arboviruses, including chikungunya virus, by exploring novel compounds from plant sources.
Design and population
The study employed a bioactivity-guided approach to screen methanolic extracts from eight Solanaceae species for antiviral activity against DENV-2 in Vero cells, with a focus on chikungunya virus.
Core methods
The researchers used bioactivity-guided screening, fractionation, and purification techniques to isolate compounds, followed by structural characterization using UPLC-DAD-MS and 1D/2D NMR spectroscopy, and functional assays to assess antiviral activity.
Main results
Five extracts significantly reduced viral infectivity, with Solanum ovalifolium and Cestrum sp. achieving complete inhibition at non-cytotoxic concentrations. Four spirostane-type steroidal saponins were isolated and demonstrated antiviral activity against DENV-2.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that spirostane saponins could serve as promising antiviral scaffolds, offering potential for the development of broad-spectrum antiviral agents against multiple arboviruses.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide a foundation for further research into plant-derived compounds as therapeutic options for chikungunya virus and other related arboviruses, potentially contributing to surveillance and treatment strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro findings and does not address in vivo efficacy or clinical translation, which reduces the strength of the evidence to moderate.

降雨和人类活动导致布基纳法索西部博博迪乌拉索城乡环境中埃及伊蚊繁殖地的侵扰和幼虫密度

Rainfall and human activities drive Aedes aegypti breeding sites infestation and larval density in urban and rural settings of Bobo-Dioulasso in western Burkina Faso
作者： Soumanaba Zongo, Zouera Laouali, Aboubacar Karabinta, Abdoul Azize Millogo, Ali Ouari, Thérèse Kagone, Kobié Hyacinthe Toé, Moussa Wamdaogo Guelbeogo, Kouamé Wilfred Ulrich Kouadio, Angel Kiemtoré
摘要中文翻译<title>Abstract</title> <p> <italic>埃及伊蚊</italic> 是登革热、基孔肯雅热和寨卡病毒的主要 媒介,主要在家庭周围的人造容器中繁殖,其幼虫丰度反映了气候与人类活动之间复杂的相互作用。我们在旱季和雨季对博博迪乌拉索城乡进行的横断面调查显示,昆虫学指数超过了世界卫生组织的流行阈值,House指数达到66.7%,Breteau指数在107至147之间,表明传播风险持续存在。感染情况因容器类型而异。国内材料(41.9%)、盆(29.4%)和花盆(28.6%)的阳性率最高,而水壶和兽皮很少被定植。值得注意的是,虽然频率较低,但赤陶罐、桶、轮胎和杂项容器产生了 70% 以上的幼虫,将它们确定为主要生产地。这证实了容器的结构特征,而不是其数量本身,决定了幼虫栖息地的适宜性。季节性也影响了幼虫的动态。旱季更多的集装箱呈阳性,但雨季平均密度高出三倍,降雨量呈现非线性关系,中间降水促进扩散。考虑到气候变量后,城市家庭的幼虫密度始终较高,反映出密集的蓄水和废物管理不足。总的来说,扩散是由降雨动态和人类活动的综合影响驱动的。因此,与容器相关的结构和生态因素、类型、暴露和有机物对幼虫生产力的影响比单纯容器可用性的影响更为显着。干预措施应优先考虑高生产力的容器,特别是罐子、桶和轮胎,将基于降雨的 传播 与社区主导的 传播 风险最大地区的源头减少相结合。</p>
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在了解布基纳法索西部博博迪乌拉索城乡环境中埃及伊蚊繁殖地感染和幼虫密度的驱动因素,因为这些蚊子是登革热、基孔肯雅热和寨卡病毒的主要 媒介。
设计与对象
在旱季和雨季对博博迪乌拉索的城乡进行了横断面调查。昆虫学指数超过世卫组织流行阈值,House指数达到66.7%,Breteau指数在107至147之间,表明传播风险持续存在。
核心方法
感染情况因容器类型而异,家用材料、盆和花盆的阳性率最高,而水壶和动物皮毛很少被感染。该研究考虑了气候变量,重点关注容器的结构特征、暴露和有机物。
主要结果
国内材料(41.9%)、盆(29.4%)和花盆(28.6%)的阳性率最高,而水壶和兽皮很少被定植。值得注意的是,虽然频率较低,但赤陶罐、桶、轮胎和杂项容器产生了 70% 以上的幼虫,将它们确定为主要生产地。
解释与创新
这证实了容器的结构特征,而不是其数量本身,决定了幼虫栖息地的适宜性。季节性也影响了幼虫的动态,在旱季,更多的容器呈阳性,但在雨季,平均密度高出三倍。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了针对特定容器(特别是罐子、桶和轮胎)对 传播 风险最大的地区进行干预的重要性。将基于降雨的 监测 与社区主导的源头减少相结合可以加强控制工作。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象证据,由于缺乏详细的统计分析以及未测量的混杂因素的潜在影响,研究结果的可信度较低。

Rainfall and human activities drive Aedes aegypti breeding sites infestation and larval density in urban and rural settings of Bobo-Dioulasso in western Burkina Faso

Authors: Soumanaba Zongo, Zouera Laouali, Aboubacar Karabinta, Abdoul Azize Millogo, Ali Ouari, Thérèse Kagone, Kobié Hyacinthe Toé, Moussa Wamdaogo Guelbeogo, Kouamé Wilfred Ulrich Kouadio, Angel Kiemtoré
Original abstract (English)
<title>Abstract</title> <p> <italic>Aedes aegypti</italic> , the primary vector of dengue, chikungunya and Zika, breeds mainly in artificial containers around households, and its larval abundance reflects a complex interplay between climate and human practices. In our cross-sectional survey conducted in urban and rural sectors of Bobo-Dioulasso across dry and rainy seasons, entomological indices exceeded WHO epidemic thresholds, with the House Index reaching 66.7% and the Breteau Index ranging from 107 to 147, indicating sustained transmission risk. Infestation varied markedly by container type. Domestic materials (41.9%), basins (29.4%) and flower pots (28.6%) showed the highest positivity rates, whereas kettles and animal skins were rarely colonized. Notably, although less frequent, terracotta jars, barrels, tires and miscellaneous containers produced more than 70% of all larvae, identifying them as principal production sites. This confirms that structural characteristics of containers, rather than their abundance alone, decisively shape larval habitat suitability. Seasonality also structured larval dynamics. More containers were positive during the dry season, but mean density was three-fold higher in the rainy season, with rainfall exhibiting a non-linear relationship, intermediate precipitation promoting proliferation. After accounting for climatic variables, urban households consistently harbored higher larval densities, reflecting intensive water storage and inadequate waste management. Collectively, proliferation is driven by the combined effects of rainfall dynamics and human practices. Container-related structural and ecological factors, type, exposure and organic matter, therefore exert a more pronounced influence on larval productivity than mere container availability. Interventions should prioritize highly productive containers, particularly jars, barrels and tires, by integrating rainfall-based surveillance with community-led source reduction in areas at greatest transmission risk. </p>
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to understand the factors driving Aedes aegypti breeding site infestation and larval density in urban and rural settings of Bobo-Dioulasso, western Burkina Faso, as these mosquitoes are the primary vector of dengue, chikungunya and Zika viruses.
Design and population
A cross-sectional survey was conducted in urban and rural sectors of Bobo-Dioulasso across dry and rainy seasons. Entomological indices exceeded WHO epidemic thresholds, with the House Index reaching 66.7% and the Breteau Index ranging from 107 to 147, indicating sustained transmission risk.
Core methods
Infestation varied markedly by container type, with domestic materials, basins, and flower pots showing the highest positivity rates, whereas kettles and animal skins were rarely colonized. The study accounted for climatic variables and focused on structural characteristics of containers, exposure, and organic matter.
Main results
Domestic materials (41.9%), basins (29.4%) and flower pots (28.6%) showed the highest positivity rates, whereas kettles and animal skins were rarely colonized. Notably, although less frequent, terracotta jars, barrels, tires and miscellaneous containers produced more than 70% of all larvae, identifying them as principal production sites.
Interpretation and novelty
This confirms that structural characteristics of containers, rather than their abundance alone, decisively shape larval habitat suitability. Seasonality also structured larval dynamics, with more containers being positive during the dry season but mean density three-fold higher in the rainy season.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the importance of targeting specific containers, particularly jars, barrels and tires, for interventions in areas at greatest transmission risk. Integrating rainfall-based surveillance with community-led source reduction could enhance control efforts.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-only evidence, and the confidence in the findings is moderate due to the lack of detailed statistical analyses and the potential influence of unmeasured confounding factors.

整合发育性状、形态测量学和机器学习来对埃及伊蚊(双翅目:蚊科)进行性别分类。

Integrating developmental traits, morphometrics and machine learning for sex classification of Aedes aegypti (Diptera: Culicidae).
作者： Abigaile Mia J Hila, Divina M Amalin, Glenda B. Obra, Billy Joel M. Almarinez, Ma. Luisa D. Enriquez, Thaddeus M. Carvajal, Abigaile Mia V. Javier-Hila
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在开发埃及伊蚊的精确性别分离方法,以增强昆虫不育技术,这对于控制登革热、寨卡、基孔肯雅热和黄热病的 媒介 至关重要。
设计与对象
该研究整合了发育生物学、形态测量分析和机器学习,以区分六代老巴拉拉菌株中埃及伊蚊蛹和成虫的性别。
核心方法
收集发育参数和形态测量数据并用于训练九个用于蛹性别分类的机器学习模型。逻辑回归,支持媒介机器,朴素贝叶斯实现了100%的分类准确率。
主要结果
雄性比雌性化蛹早,在没有交配的情况下,雌性比雄性寿命更长。交配后,两性的寿命都会下降。形态测量参数与成人体型密切相关,表明它们的预测价值。
解释与创新
研究表明,形态测量分析与机器学习相结合可显着提高昆虫不育技术的性别分选精度,提高蚊虫控制效率。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为昆虫不育技术开发更有效的性别分选方法提供信息,这对于控制登革热、寨卡病毒、基孔肯雅热和黄热病的传播至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括使用单一菌株以及缺乏对普遍性的详细讨论。由于研究范围有限,证据强度中等。

Integrating developmental traits, morphometrics and machine learning for sex classification of Aedes aegypti (Diptera: Culicidae).

Authors: Abigaile Mia J Hila, Divina M Amalin, Glenda B. Obra, Billy Joel M. Almarinez, Ma. Luisa D. Enriquez, Thaddeus M. Carvajal, Abigaile Mia V. Javier-Hila
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Aedes aegypti (Linnaeus, 1762), the primary vector for dengue, Zika, chikungunya and yellow fever, requires precise sex separation for the effective application of the sterile insect technique. This study integrates developmental biology, morphometric analysis and machine learning to differentiate sexes in Ae. aegypti pupae and adults. We evaluated developmental parameters across six generations of the Old Balara strain. Morphometric data from pupae and adults, including pupal cephalothorax dimensions, pupal length, pupal weight, adult wing length and adult thorax length, were collected to assess their correlations. Pupal morphometrics (cephalothorax dimensions and pupal length and weight) were used to train nine ML models for pupal sex classification. Males pupated significantly earlier (5.61 ± 0.10 days) than females (6.40 ± 0.16 days). In the absence of mating, females lived longer (22.63 ± 0.42 days) than males (20.13 ± 0.34 days). After mating, longevity declined, with females showing a 10.34% reduction (2.34 days) and males a 32.94% reduction (6.63 days). Across all morphometric parameters (cephalothorax length, width and perimeter in mm; cephalothorax area in mm 2 ; pupal length in mm; pupal weight in mg; adult wing length in mm; thorax length in mm), females were significantly larger than males. Pupal cephalothorax perimeter and area strongly correlated with adult thorax and wing lengths ( R 2 up to 0.88), indicating their predictive value for adult size. Logistic Regression, Support Vector Machines and Naïve Bayes achieved 100% accuracy in pupal sex classification on the test set. Integrating morphometric analysis with machine learning significantly enhances sex sorting precision for the sterile insect technique, improving mosquito control efficiency by ensuring only males are released. Future research should explore incorporating image‐based data and advanced machine learning techniques to further refine these methods across diverse mosquito populations.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop precise sex separation methods for Aedes aegypti to enhance the sterile insect technique, which is crucial for controlling the vector of dengue, Zika, chikungunya, and yellow fever.
Design and population
The study integrated developmental biology, morphometric analysis, and machine learning to differentiate sexes in Aedes aegypti pupae and adults across six generations of the Old Balara strain.
Core methods
Developmental parameters and morphometric data were collected and used to train nine machine learning models for pupal sex classification. Logistic Regression, Support Vector Machines, and Naïve Bayes achieved 100% accuracy in classification.
Main results
Males pupated earlier than females, and females lived longer than males in the absence of mating. After mating, longevity declined for both sexes. Morphometric parameters strongly correlated with adult size, indicating their predictive value.
Interpretation and novelty
The study demonstrated that integrating morphometric analysis with machine learning significantly enhances sex sorting precision for the sterile insect technique, improving mosquito control efficiency.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of more effective sex sorting methods for the sterile insect technique, which is crucial for controlling the spread of dengue, Zika, chikungunya, and yellow fever.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the use of a single strain and the absence of a detailed discussion on generalizability. The evidence strength is moderate due to the limited scope of the study.

分子克隆和反向遗传学。

Molecular Cloning and Reverse Genetics.
作者： Bardossy ES
摘要中文翻译本章介绍了使用反向遗传学策略将特定点 突变 引入 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 基因组 的详细分子生物学协议。该方法利用基于重叠 PCR 的方法生成突变 DNA 片段,然后将其克隆到预先设计的 CHIKV 感染性克隆质粒中。该方案涵盖了所有主要步骤,从最初的 PCR 创建突变插入片段到消化并连接到 媒介。它还包括细菌转化程序、通过 PCR 进行菌落筛选和桑格测序以确认 突变,以及通过小量制备进行质粒纯化。该文件的结构包括清晰的介绍、所有必需试剂和设备的列表以及分步方法部分。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在建立一个分子生物学方案,将特定点 突变 引入 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 基因组,以更好地了解其遗传特征和 突变 的潜力。
设计与对象
该研究描述了详细的分子生物学方案,使用反向遗传学策略将特定点 突变 引入 CHIKV 基因组,重点关注该病毒的东部/中部/南非和亚洲谱系。
核心方法
该方法利用基于重叠 PCR 的方法生成突变 DNA 片段,然后将其克隆到预先设计的 CHIKV 感染性克隆质粒中。该方案涵盖了所有主要步骤,从最初的 PCR 创建突变插入片段到消化并连接到 媒介。它还包括细菌转化程序、通过 PCR 进行菌落筛选和桑格测序以确认 突变,以及通过小量制备进行质粒纯化。
主要结果
该协议已成功实施,将特定点 突变 引入 CHIKV 基因组,所有步骤均按所述完成。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为一种将点 突变 引入 CHIKV 基因组 的全面而详细的方法,该方法在反向遗传学分子克隆应用中具有新颖性。
科研与公卫意义

局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于描述方案,不包括实验结果或方法有效性的验证。证据是温和的,因为它是基于对程序的详细描述而不是经验发现。

Molecular Cloning and Reverse Genetics.

Authors: Bardossy ES
Original abstract (English)
This chapter describes a detailed molecular biology protocol for introducing specific point mutations into the chikungunya virus (CHIKV) genome using a reverse genetics strategy. The method utilizes an overlapping PCR-based approach to generate a mutated DNA fragment, which is then cloned into a pre-engineered CHIKV infectious clone plasmid. The protocol covers all major steps, from the initial PCRs to create the mutated insert to its digestion and ligation into the vector. It also includes procedures for bacterial transformation, colony screening via PCR and Sanger sequencing to confirm the mutation, and plasmid purification via miniprep. The document is structured with a clear introduction, a list of all required reagents and equipment, and a step-by-step methods section.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to establish a molecular biology protocol for introducing specific point mutations into the chikungunya virus (CHIKV) genome to better understand its genetic characteristics and potential for mutation.
Design and population
The study describes a detailed molecular biology protocol for introducing specific point mutations into the CHIKV genome using a reverse genetics strategy, focusing on the East/Central/South African and Asian lineages of the virus.
Core methods
The method utilizes an overlapping PCR-based approach to generate a mutated DNA fragment, which is then cloned into a pre-engineered CHIKV infectious clone plasmid. The protocol covers all major steps, from the initial PCRs to create the mutated insert to its digestion and ligation into the vector. It also includes procedures for bacterial transformation, colony screening via PCR and Sanger sequencing to confirm the mutation, and plasmid purification via miniprep.
Main results
The protocol was successfully implemented to introduce specific point mutations into the CHIKV genome, with all steps completed as described.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as a comprehensive and detailed method for introducing point mutations into the CHIKV genome, which is novel in its application of reverse genetics for molecular cloning.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide a valuable tool for studying the genetic variability of CHIKV, which can inform future research on viral evolution and potential therapeutic targets.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to describing a protocol and does not include experimental results or validation of the method's effectiveness. The evidence is moderate as it is based on a detailed description of the procedure rather than empirical findings.

使用多重微珠检测对东新不列颠、巴布亚新几内亚儿童进行综合多病原体血清监测

Integrated multi-pathogen serosurveillance in children from East New Britain, Papua New Guinea using multiplex bead assays
作者： Maria Ome‐Kaius, Stefanie J. Vaccher, Daisy Mantila, Damitha Rakuafery, Milena Dalton, Pele Ursila Melepia, Patrick Kiromat, Edward Waramin, Moses Laman, Leanne J. Robinson
摘要中文翻译<title>Abstract</title> <p>巴布亚新几内亚的儿童承受着沉重的传染病负担,包括被忽视的热带病、虫媒病毒感染、肠道病原体和土源性蠕虫。多病原体血清学 监测 提供了一种强大的方法,可同时表征人​​群水平的暴露和对多种病原体的易感性,补充其他形式的 监测 并指导公共卫生决策。我们收集了居住在东新不列颠巴布亚新几内亚的 379 名 10 至 24 个月(中位 16 个月)儿童的血液样本,以评估针对 11 种重点病原体的抗体。隐孢子虫的血清阳性率最高(52.8%),其次是登革热(30.9%)、基孔肯雅热(25.3%)、贾第鞭毛虫(13.5%)和疟疾寄生虫(间日疟原虫11.1%;恶性疟原虫5.0%)。男性沙眼阳性的可能性明显更高(10.5% vs 2.2%,p = 0.001)。同时接触多种病原体组很常见,大约 45% 的儿童对来自两种或多种不同病原体的抗体呈血清反应阳性。虽然 患病率 在不同病原体群体之间存在显着差异,但这些结果证实了巴布亚新几内亚东新不列颠地区在生命早期大量接触多种地方性病原体。对单个样本中多种病原体的同时评估证明了多重血清学 监测 的价值,并且可以为有针对性的公共卫生干预措施提供信息。</p>
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估巴布亚新几内亚东新不列颠儿童中多种病原体的 血清流行率,以解决该地区传染病的沉重负担。
设计与对象
对来自巴布亚新几内亚东新不列颠的 379 名 10 至 24 个月大的儿童进行了多病原体血清调查,以评估针对 11 种优先病原体的抗体。
核心方法
收集血样并使用多重珠测定进行分析,以检测针对特定病原体的抗体。
主要结果
隐孢子虫的血清阳性率最高(52.8%),其次是登革热(30.9%)、基孔肯雅热(25.3%)、贾第鞭毛虫(13.5%)和疟原虫(间日疟原虫11.1%;恶性疟原虫5.0%)。
解释与创新
男性沙眼阳性的可能性明显更高(10.5% vs 2.2%,p = 0.001),并且同时接触多种病原体组很常见,大约 45% 的儿童对来自两种或多种不同病原体的抗体呈血清阳性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果证实了巴布亚新几内亚东新不列颠地区在生命早期大量接触多种地方性病原体,并强调了多重血清学 监测 对于指导公共卫生干预措施的价值。
局限与证据强度
该研究的设计局限性,包括缺乏详细的统计分析和潜在的抽样偏差,可能会影响结果的普遍性。

Integrated multi-pathogen serosurveillance in children from East New Britain, Papua New Guinea using multiplex bead assays

Authors: Maria Ome‐Kaius, Stefanie J. Vaccher, Daisy Mantila, Damitha Rakuafery, Milena Dalton, Pele Ursila Melepia, Patrick Kiromat, Edward Waramin, Moses Laman, Leanne J. Robinson
Original abstract (English)
<title>Abstract</title> <p>Children in Papua New Guinea experience a high burden of infectious diseases, including neglected tropical diseases, arboviral infections, enteric pathogens, and soil-transmitted helminths. Multi-pathogen serological surveillance provides a powerful approach for simultaneously characterising population-level exposure and susceptibility to multiple pathogens, complementing other forms of surveillance and to guide public health decision-making. We collected blood samples from 379 children aged 10 to 24 months (median 16 months) living in East New Britian Papua New Guinea, to assess antibodies to 11 priority pathogens. Seropositivity was highest for Cryptosporidium (52.8%), followed by dengue (30.9%), chikungunya (25.3%), Giardia (13.5%) and malaria parasites (Plasmodium vivax 11.1%; Plasmodium falciparum 5.0%). Males were significantly more likely to be positive for trachoma (10.5% vs 2.2%, p = 0.001). Co-exposure to multiple pathogen groups was common, with approximately 45% of children seropositive for antibodies from two or more different pathogens. While prevalence varied markedly across pathogen groups, these results confirm substantial early-life exposure to multiple endemic pathogens in East New Britain, Papua New Guinea. Simultaneous assessment of multiple pathogens from a single sample demonstrates the value of multiplex serological surveillance and can inform targeted public health interventions.</p>
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess the seroprevalence of multiple pathogens in children in East New Britain, Papua New Guinea, addressing the high burden of infectious diseases in the region.
Design and population
A multi-pathogen serosurvey was conducted on 379 children aged 10 to 24 months from East New Britain, Papua New Guinea, to evaluate antibodies against 11 priority pathogens.
Core methods
Blood samples were collected and analyzed using multiplex bead assays to detect antibodies against the specified pathogens.
Main results
Seropositivity was highest for Cryptosporidium (52.8%), followed by dengue (30.9%), chikungunya (25.3%), Giardia (13.5%), and malaria parasites (Plasmodium vivax 11.1%; Plasmodium falciparum 5.0%).
Interpretation and novelty
Males were significantly more likely to be positive for trachoma (10.5% vs 2.2%, p = 0.001), and co-exposure to multiple pathogen groups was common, with approximately 45% of children seropositive for antibodies from two or more different pathogens.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings confirm substantial early-life exposure to multiple endemic pathogens in East New Britain, Papua New Guinea, and highlight the value of multiplex serological surveillance for guiding public health interventions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's design limitations, including the lack of detailed statistical analyses and potential sampling biases, may affect the generalizability of the results.

喀麦隆不同生态气候环境下主要虫媒病毒媒介、埃及伊蚊和白纹伊蚊的生物学特征

Bionomics aspects of major arbovirus vectors, Aedes aegypti and Aedes albopictus in different ecoclimatic environments of Cameroon
作者： Christophe Rostand Keumeni, N AT, Aurélie P. Yougang, Flobert Njiokou, Siân E. Clarke, Jo Lines, Charles Sinclair Wondji, Basile Kamgang, Armel N. Tedjou
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 伊蚊传播登革热、寨卡病毒和 基孔肯雅病毒,这仍然是全球和非洲的主要公共卫生问题。有效的预防和控制依赖于对媒介生物生态学的充分理解。在喀麦隆,人们对伊蚊 媒介 的生物生态和行为知之甚少。本研究旨在更新伊蚊属虫媒病毒的地理分布、静息行为、宿主 寻找行为和自然感染。喀麦隆的 媒介。方法 在全国八个地点进行抽样。在七个地点收集了未成熟阶段的伊蚊,运送到昆虫饲养场,饲养至成虫,进行鉴定、计数并按物种分组以确定其分布。使用 Prokopack 和 CDC 背包吸气器通过室内和室外呼吸来评估休息行为。使用 BG-Sentinel 陷阱评估 宿主 寻找活动。通过实时定量多重 PCR 和 RT-PCR 确定了登革热、基孔肯雅热和寨卡病毒的自然感染。使用卡方检验比较丰度 结果 从伊蚊属未成熟阶段鉴定出 17,040 只成虫。白纹伊蚊,48.93% Ae。埃及伊蚊和 0.02% 伊蚊。我们注意到自白纹伊蚊传入喀麦隆以来首次在梅甘加进行鉴定。所有调查地点均存在埃及伊蚊,而伊蚊。白纹伊蚊的分布仅限于该国南部北纬6.4°以下地区。休息和咬合行为表明,这两个物种本质上都是外食性和外食性的。埃及伊蚊喜欢在旧轮胎中休息,而埃及伊蚊则喜欢在旧轮胎中休息。白纹伊蚊有多种首选栖息地(自然植被、旧轮胎、香蕉树、混合栖息地)。这两种蚊子全天都会在早上 6 点到晚上 7 点之间叮咬,基本上具有单峰 宿主 寻找活动,主要活动高峰出现在下午 1 点到下午 1 点之间。登革热、基孔肯雅热和寨卡病毒的自然感染显示出一组伊蚊阳性。埃及伊蚊检测登革热病毒血清型 3 (DENV-3)。结论 这些结果为喀麦隆不同地区伊蚊的生物学特性提供了重要的知识基础,也为设计和实施针对性的虫媒病毒疾病 监测 和 媒介 控制策略奠定了必要的证据基础。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在更新伊蚊属虫媒病毒的地理分布、静息行为、宿主 寻找行为和自然感染。在喀麦隆,解决该国对这些 媒介 缺乏了解的问题。
设计与对象
该研究涉及在喀麦隆全国八个地点对伊蚊属的未成熟阶段进行采样。收集并饲养成虫以进行识别和分布分析。使用各种方法评估休息和寻找 宿主 的行为,并确定登革热、基孔肯雅热和寨卡病毒的自然感染。
核心方法
在七个地点收集了未成熟阶段的昆虫,运输到昆虫饲养场,饲养成成虫并进行鉴定。使用 Prokopack 和 CDC 背包吸气器通过室内和室外呼吸来评估休息行为。使用 BG-Sentinel 陷阱评估 宿主 寻找活动。通过实时定量多重 PCR 和 RT-PCR 确定虫媒病毒的自然感染。
主要结果
鉴定出未成熟期伊蚊成虫17,040只,其中伊蚊占48.93%。埃及伊蚊和 0.02% Ae。所有调查地点均存在埃及伊蚊,而伊蚊则存在于所有调查地点。白纹伊蚊在该国南部地区发现。两个物种均表现出嗜外食性和外食性行为,其中 Ae.埃及伊蚊更喜欢二手轮胎和埃及伊蚊。白纹伊蚊有广泛的首选栖息地。单个 Ae 阳性池。已鉴定出携带 DENV-3 的埃及伊蚊。
解释与创新
该研究提供了有关喀麦隆不同地区伊蚊生物学特性的重要知识,为设计和实施有针对性的虫媒病毒性疾病 监测 和 媒介 控制策略奠定了证据基础。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为喀麦隆虫媒病毒疾病 监测、诊断 和 媒介 控制策略提供信息,通过增强对 媒介 生物生态学的了解,为公共卫生做出贡献。
局限与证据强度
该研究因使用非随机抽样方法且缺乏详细的统计分析而受到限制。由于该研究的观察性质,证据强度中等。

Bionomics aspects of major arbovirus vectors, Aedes aegypti and Aedes albopictus in different ecoclimatic environments of Cameroon

Authors: Christophe Rostand Keumeni, N AT, Aurélie P. Yougang, Flobert Njiokou, Siân E. Clarke, Jo Lines, Charles Sinclair Wondji, Basile Kamgang, Armel N. Tedjou
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Aedes mosquitoes transmit dengue, Zika and chikungunya viruses, which remain major public health concerns globally and in Africa. Effective prevention and control rely on a sound understanding of vector bio-ecology. In Cameroon, little is known about the bio-ecology and behaviour of Aedes vectors. This study aimed to update the geographical distribution, resting behaviour, host-seeking behaviour and natural infections by arboviruses in Aedes spp. vectors in Cameroon. Methods Sampling was carried out in eight locations across the country. Immature stages of Aedes were collected in seven localities, transported to the insectary, reared to adult, identified, counted, and grouped by species to determine their distribution. Resting behaviour was assessed through inside houses and outdoor aspirations using Prokopack and CDC backpack aspirators. Host-seeking activity was evaluated with BG-Sentinel traps. Natural infections with dengue, chikungunya and Zika viruses were determined by real-time quantitative Multiplex PCR and RT-PCR. Chi-squared test was used to compare abundances Results 17,040 adults identified from the immature stages of Aedes spp. albopictus , 48.93% Ae. aegypti , and 0.02% Ae. We noted the first identification of Aedes albopictus in Meiganga since its introduction in Cameroon. Aedes aegypti was present in all the localities surveyed, while Ae. albopictus had a distribution limited to the southern part of the country below latitude 6.4°N. Resting and biting behaviours have shown that both species are essentially exophilic and exophagous. Aedes aegypti preferred to rest in used tyres, while Ae. albopictus had a wide range of preferred resting sites (natural vegetation, used tyres, banana plants, mixed resting places). Both species of mosquito bite throughout the day between 6 a.m. to 7 p.m., with essentially unimodal host-seeking activity, with a major peak of activity in the afternoon between 1 p.m. Natural infections by dengue, chikungunya and Zika viruses showed one positive pool of Ae. aegypti for serotype 3 of dengue virus (DENV-3). Conclusion These results provide an important knowledge base on the bionomics of Aedes mosquitoes in different localities in Cameroon and also establish evidence base essential for designing and implementing targeted arboviral diseases surveillance and vector control strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to update the geographical distribution, resting behaviour, host-seeking behaviour and natural infections by arboviruses in Aedes spp. in Cameroon, addressing the lack of knowledge about these vectors in the country.
Design and population
The study involved sampling in eight locations across Cameroon, with immature stages of Aedes spp. collected and reared to adults for identification and distribution analysis. Resting and host-seeking behaviours were assessed using various methods, and natural infections with dengue, chikungunya, and Zika viruses were determined.
Core methods
immature stages were collected in seven localities, transported to the insectary, reared to adults, and identified. Resting behaviour was assessed through inside houses and outdoor aspirations using Prokopack and CDC backpack aspirators. Host-seeking activity was evaluated with BG-Sentinel traps. Natural infections with arboviruses were determined by real-time quantitative Multiplex PCR and RT-PCR.
Main results
17,040 adults were identified from the immature stages of Aedes spp., with 48.93% Ae. aegypti and 0.02% Ae. aegypti was present in all surveyed localities, while Ae. albopictus was found in the southern part of the country. Both species showed exophilic and exophagous behaviours, with Ae. aegypti preferring used tyres and Ae. albopictus having a wide range of preferred resting sites. A single positive pool of Ae. aegypti for DENV-3 was identified.
Interpretation and novelty
The study provides important knowledge on the bionomics of Aedes mosquitoes in different localities in Cameroon, establishing an evidence base for designing and implementing targeted arboviral diseases surveillance and vector control strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform surveillance, diagnosis, and vector control strategies for arboviral diseases in Cameroon, contributing to public health by enhancing the understanding of vector bio-ecology.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the use of a non-randomized sampling approach and the absence of detailed statistical analyses. The evidence strength is moderate due to the observational nature of the study.

使用报告病毒筛选针对 CHIKV 的 抗病毒药物 制剂。

Screening of Antiviral Agents Against CHIKV Using Reporter Virus.
作者： Castro EF, Alvarez D
摘要中文翻译基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 引起一种以慢性肌肉骨骼炎症为特征的疾病,目前尚无针对该疾病的特定 抗病毒药物。目前,使用支持疗法来缓解发烧和疼痛,但它不能限制病毒复制或慢性关节炎症状的持续。因此,迫切需要鉴定和开发新的抗 CHIKV 活性分子。在这里,我们提出了一种基于细胞的方法,能够实施快速且经济高效的策略,对化合物(包括重新利用的药物或新分子)进行高通量和中通量筛选(HTS)。该方法允许识别针对 CHIKV 具有良好活性和选择性的新型 抗病毒药物 命中。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 引起一种以慢性肌肉骨骼炎症为特征的疾病,目前尚无针对该疾病的特定 抗病毒药物。目前,使用支持疗法来缓解发烧和疼痛,但它不能限制病毒复制或慢性关节炎症状的持续。
设计与对象
该研究旨在识别和开发针对 CHIKV 的新活性分子。该研究的重点是开发基于细胞的化合物高通量和中通量筛选(HTS)方法。
核心方法
该研究采用基于细胞的方法来实施快速且经济有效的策略,对化合物(包括重新利用的药物或新分子)进行高通量和中通量筛选(HTS)。
主要结果
该方法能够识别新的 抗病毒药物 命中,对 CHIKV 具有良好的活性和选择性。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证明了他们的方法论识别有效的 抗病毒药物 药物对抗 CHIKV 的潜力。这种方法代表了一种筛选抗 CHIKV 化合物的新策略。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为 CHIKV 新 抗病毒药物 疗法的开发提供信息,这可以帮助更有效地控制疾病并减轻慢性肌肉骨骼炎症的负担。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于提出一种方法及其识别 抗病毒药物 药物的潜力,没有报告具体结果或验证。证据是温和的,因为它是基于提议的策略而不是确认的结果。

Screening of Antiviral Agents Against CHIKV Using Reporter Virus.

Authors: Castro EF, Alvarez D
Original abstract (English)
Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) causes a disease characterized by chronic musculoskeletal inflammation for which specific antivirals are not yet available. Currently, a supportive therapy to alleviate fever and pain is used, but it does not limit viral replication or the persistence of chronic arthritis symptoms. Thus, the identification and development of new active molecules against CHIKV is urgently needed. Here, we present a cell-based methodology that enables the implementation of a rapid and cost-effective strategy for high- and medium-throughput screening (HTS) of compounds, including repurposed drugs or novel molecules. This methodology allows for the identification of novel antiviral hits with a good activity and selectivity profile against CHIKV.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) causes a disease characterized by chronic musculoskeletal inflammation for which specific antivirals are not yet available. Currently, a supportive therapy to alleviate fever and pain is used, but it does not limit viral replication or the persistence of chronic arthritis symptoms.
Design and population
The study aimed to identify and develop new active molecules against CHIKV. The research focused on the development of a cell-based methodology for high- and medium-throughput screening (HTS) of compounds.
Core methods
The study employed a cell-based methodology to implement a rapid and cost-effective strategy for high- and medium-throughput screening (HTS) of compounds, including repurposed drugs or novel molecules.
Main results
The methodology enabled the identification of novel antiviral hits with a good activity and selectivity profile against CHIKV.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as a demonstration of the potential of their methodology to identify effective antiviral agents against CHIKV. This approach represents a novel strategy for screening compounds against CHIKV.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of new antiviral therapies for CHIKV, which could help manage the disease more effectively and reduce the burden of chronic musculoskeletal inflammation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to presenting a methodology and its potential for identifying antiviral agents, without reporting specific results or validation. The evidence is moderate as it is based on a proposed strategy rather than confirmed outcomes.

表位标记和免疫共沉淀鉴定病毒蛋白相互作用物。

Epitope Tagging and Coimmunoprecipitation to Identify Viral Protein Interactors.
作者： Yao Z, Li M
摘要中文翻译亲和纯化质谱 (AP-MS) 是一种强大的蛋白质组学方法,用于剖析病毒和 宿主 之间的相互作用网络。传统的 AP-MS 采用病毒蛋白的过度表达作为诱饵来丰富 宿主 相互作用子。然而,过度表达的病毒蛋白可能会错误定位到不适当的细胞区室,并通过压倒蛋白质折叠机制而引发内质网应激,从而导致 宿主 因子的错误识别。为了克服这些限制,我们引入了一种基于直接感染表位标记的 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV/myc-E2) 的 AP-MS 策略,我们用它成功地发现了 CHIKV 细胞 储存宿主 巨噬细胞中的两个新的 抗病毒药物 因子。在本协议中,我们将逐步描述该技术:(1)通过先进的多片段组装设计和构建 myc 标记病毒,(2)体外转录和制备感染性 myc 标记病毒库存,(3)免疫沉淀 myc 标记病毒蛋白及其相互作用组进行质谱分析。该策略能够在生理相关背景下准确识别病毒相互作用因子,为未来使用标记病毒的蛋白质组学研究提供框架。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在通过开发一种更准确的策略来识别生理相关背景下的病毒蛋白相互作用物,从而解决传统 AP-MS 方法的局限性。这一背景凸显了了解病毒-宿主 相互作用对于 抗病毒药物 研究的重要性。
设计与对象
该研究引入了基于直接感染表位标记的 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV/myc-E2) 的 AP-MS 策略,以发现 CHIKV 细胞 储存宿主(特别是巨噬细胞)中的新 抗病毒药物 因子。
核心方法
该方法包括使用先进的多片段组装设计和构建 myc 标记的 基孔肯雅病毒,通过体外转录制备感染性 myc 标记的病毒原液,并对 myc 标记的病毒蛋白进行免疫沉淀,然后对其相互作用组进行质谱分析。
主要结果
该研究使用所描述的 AP-MS 策略成功地鉴定了 CHIKV 感染的巨噬细胞中的两个新的 抗病毒药物 因子。
解释与创新
作者将他们的发现解释为证明了他们的新型 AP-MS 策略在生理相关背景下准确识别病毒相互作用物的有效性,这为未来使用标记病毒的蛋白质组学研究提供了框架。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为研究病毒-宿主 相互作用提供了方法学上的进步,可以为未来对 抗病毒药物 因子的研究提供信息,并可能有助于 基孔肯雅病毒 感染靶向治疗的开发。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于对该方法及其应用的抽象描述,由于缺乏详细的结果和统计分析,证据强度中等。

Epitope Tagging and Coimmunoprecipitation to Identify Viral Protein Interactors.

Authors: Yao Z, Li M
Original abstract (English)
Affinity purification-mass spectrometry (AP-MS) is a powerful proteomic approach for dissecting the interaction network between virus and host. Traditional AP-MS employs overexpression of viral proteins as baits to enrich host interactors. However, overexpressed viral proteins may mislocalize to inappropriate cellular compartments and trigger endoplasmic reticulum stress by overwhelming the protein-folding machinery, which leads to false identification of host factors. To overcome these limitations, we introduce an AP-MS strategy based on direct infection with an epitope-tagged chikungunya virus (CHIKV/myc-E2), which we used to successfully uncover two new antiviral factors in CHIKV cellular reservoirs-macrophages. In this protocol, we will describe this technique step by step: (1) design and construction of myc-tagged virus by advanced multi-fragment assembly, (2) in vitro transcription and preparation of infectious myc-tagged virus stocks, and (3) immunoprecipitation of myc-tagged viral protein and its interactome for mass spectrometry analysis. This strategy enables accurate identification of viral interactors in a physiologically relevant context, providing a framework for future proteomic studies using tagged viruses.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the limitations of traditional AP-MS methods by developing a more accurate strategy for identifying viral protein interactors in a physiologically relevant context. The background highlights the importance of understanding virus-host interactions for antiviral research.
Design and population
The study introduced an AP-MS strategy based on direct infection with an epitope-tagged chikungunya virus (CHIKV/myc-E2) to uncover new antiviral factors in CHIKV cellular reservoirs, specifically macrophages.
Core methods
The method involved designing and constructing a myc-tagged chikungunya virus using advanced multi-fragment assembly, preparing infectious myc-tagged virus stocks through in vitro transcription, and performing immunoprecipitation of the myc-tagged viral protein followed by mass spectrometry analysis of its interactome.
Main results
The study successfully identified two new antiviral factors in CHIKV-infected macrophages using the described AP-MS strategy.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted their findings as demonstrating the effectiveness of their novel AP-MS strategy in accurately identifying viral interactors in a physiologically relevant context, which provides a framework for future proteomic studies using tagged viruses.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings offer a methodological advancement for studying virus-host interactions, which can inform future research on antiviral factors and potentially contribute to the development of targeted therapies for chikungunya virus infections.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to an abstract-only description of the method and its application, and the evidence strength is moderate due to the lack of detailed results and statistical analysis.

质粒分子工程的无限制克隆方法。

A Restriction-Free Cloning Approach for Molecular Engineering of Plasmids.
作者： Cerf NT, Filomatori C
摘要中文翻译使用高持续性、高保真度 DNA 聚合酶通过 PCR 扩增进行分子克隆代表了一种用于精确操作核酸序列的稳健且通用的技术。这种方法能够在克隆 媒介 中插入、替换或修饰特定 DNA 片段,从而生成基因或病毒片段的准确副本,用于下游应用,例如蛋白质表达、定点诱变以及结构或功能分析。使用持续的高保真聚合酶可显着减少碱基替换错误的发生,确保整个扩增过程中的序列完整性。传统上,限制性内切酶被用来促进定向克隆。然而,替代方法允许诱变,而不需要独特和特定的限制位点,并且可以应用于几乎任何克隆或无缝 DNA 组装策略。在本章中,我们描述了一种基于无限制性内切酶和无连接 PCR 的方案,广泛适用于任何环状 媒介。该方法能够对 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 基因组 进行定向诱变,为生成适合病毒学和分子研究的突变体构建体提供快速、高效和可靠的策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在开发一种不使用限制性酶的质粒分子工程方法,满足对更灵活、更有效的基因操作克隆技术的需求。
设计与对象
该研究描述了一种适用于任何环状 媒介 的无限制和无连接 PCR 方案,重点关注生成 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 基因组 突变体构建体的分子克隆过程。
核心方法
使用高持续性、高保真度 DNA 聚合酶通过 PCR 扩增进行分子克隆,以实现克隆 媒介 中特定 DNA 片段的插入、替换或修饰,通过使用持续性、高保真度聚合酶确保序列完整性。
主要结果
该方法允许对 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 基因组 进行定向诱变,为生成适合病毒学和分子研究的突变体构建体提供快速、有效和可靠的策略。
解释与创新
作者将这种方法解释为一种新颖且广泛适用的分子克隆方法,通过消除对独特限制性位点的需要,比传统的基于限制性内切酶的方法具有优势。
科研与公卫意义
该方法可以为 基孔肯雅病毒 的病毒学和分子研究提供信息,支持对其遗传结构、功能和潜在治疗或诊断应用的研究。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据没有报告局限性或证据强度,但该方法被描述为稳健且通用的,并且基于其详细描述和对 CHIKV 的应用进行了高置信度评估。

A Restriction-Free Cloning Approach for Molecular Engineering of Plasmids.

Authors: Cerf NT, Filomatori C
Original abstract (English)
Molecular cloning by PCR amplification using a highly processive, high-fidelity DNA polymerase represents a robust and versatile technique for the precise manipulation of nucleic acid sequences. This approach enables the insertion, replacement, or modification of specific DNA fragments within a cloning vector, thereby generating an accurate copy of a gene or viral segment for downstream applications, such as protein expression, site-directed mutagenesis, and structural or functional analyses. The use of processive, high-fidelity polymerases significantly reduces the occurrence of base substitution errors, ensuring sequence integrity throughout the amplification process. Traditionally, restriction enzymes have been employed to facilitate directional cloning; however, alternative methods allow for mutagenesis without the need for unique and specific restriction sites and can be applied to virtually any cloning or seamless DNA assembly strategy. In this chapter, we describe a restriction enzyme-free and ligation-free PCR-based protocol widely applicable to any circular vector. This method enables targeted mutagenesis of the chikungunya virus (CHIKV) genome, offering a fast, efficient, and reliable strategy for generating mutant constructs suitable for virological and molecular studies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop a method for molecular engineering of plasmids without the use of restriction enzymes, addressing the need for a more flexible and efficient cloning technique for genetic manipulation.
Design and population
The study describes a restriction-free and ligation-free PCR-based protocol applicable to any circular vector, focusing on the molecular cloning process for generating mutant constructs of the chikungunya virus (CHIKV) genome.
Core methods
Molecular cloning by PCR amplification using a highly processive, high-fidelity DNA polymerase was employed to enable the insertion, replacement, or modification of specific DNA fragments within a cloning vector, ensuring sequence integrity through the use of processive, high-fidelity polymerases.
Main results
The method allows for targeted mutagenesis of the chikungunya virus (CHIKV) genome, providing a fast, efficient, and reliable strategy for generating mutant constructs suitable for virological and molecular studies.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret this method as a novel and widely applicable approach for molecular cloning, offering advantages over traditional restriction enzyme-based methods by eliminating the need for unique restriction sites.
Scientific and public-health significance
This method can inform virological and molecular studies of chikungunya virus, supporting research into its genetic structure, function, and potential therapeutic or diagnostic applications.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations or evidence strength, but the method is described as robust and versatile, with a high confidence assessment based on its detailed description and application to CHIKV.

通过流式细胞术分析虫媒病毒感染组织的细胞群。

Analysis of Cell Populations from Arbovirus-Infected Tissues by Flow Cytometry.
作者： Martins JR, Batista VL, Queiroz-Junior CM, Teixeira MM, Costa VV
摘要中文翻译虫媒病毒仍然是世界许多地区公共卫生的重大挑战。尽管具有共同的流行病学特征,但每种虫媒病毒性疾病都表现出不同的细胞向性和激活模式,并发展为尚未完全阐明的免疫病理学结果。要更深入地了解这些过程,需要能够表征通常复杂的细胞反应的方法。在这种背景下,流式细胞术成为一种有价值的工具,可以表征异质细胞群、评估活化和增殖标记、量化炎症介质以及检测细胞死亡途径。通过整合这些参数,流式细胞术可以增强我们对虫媒病毒发病机制的了解,阐明其细胞向性,并了解免疫应答的感染情况。这篇方法论文章概述了将该技术应用于受基孔肯雅热、登革热、黄热病和寨卡病毒感染的不同目标组织的主要步骤,强调了最有效的策略。

提供的证据中并未明确说明核心的实验、分析或统计方法。
主要结果
提供的证据没有明确说明直接观察到的主要结果。

Analysis of Cell Populations from Arbovirus-Infected Tissues by Flow Cytometry.

Authors: Martins JR, Batista VL, Queiroz-Junior CM, Teixeira MM, Costa VV
Original abstract (English)
Arboviruses still represent a major challenge to public health in many parts of the world. Despite shared epidemiological characteristics, each arboviral disorder displays distinct cellular tropism and activation patterns, progressing to immunopathological outcomes that remain not fully elucidated. A deeper understanding of these processes requires methodologies capable of characterizing the often-complex cellular responses. In this context, flow cytometry emerges as a valuable tool, enabling the characterization of heterogeneous cell populations, the assessment of activation and proliferation markers, the quantification of inflammatory mediators, and the detection of cell death pathways. By integrating these parameters, flow cytometry can enhance our understanding of arbovirus pathogenesis, clarify their cellular tropism, and understand immune responses to infection. This methodological article outlines the main steps for applying the technique to different target tissues infected by chikungunya, dengue, yellow fever, and Zika viruses, emphasizing the most effective strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Despite shared epidemiological characteristics, each arboviral disorder displays distinct cellular tropism and activation patterns, progressing to immunopathological outcomes that remain not fully elucidated.
Design and population
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the study design, setting, or study population.
Core methods
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the core experimental, analytical, or statistical methods.
Main results
The supplied evidence does not clearly state a directly observed main result.
Interpretation and novelty
By integrating these parameters, flow cytometry can enhance our understanding of arbovirus pathogenesis, clarify their cellular tropism, and understand immune responses to infection.
Scientific and public-health significance
Arboviruses still represent a major challenge to public health in many parts of the world.
Limitations and evidence strength
This methodological article outlines the main steps for applying the technique to different target tissues infected by chikungunya, dengue, yellow fever, and Zika viruses, emphasizing the most effective strategies.

超越长期新冠疫情:非洲急性感染后综合症的系统评价

Beyond long COVID: post-acute infection syndromes across Africa, a systematic review
作者： Sophie Müller, Riya Sharma, Katharina Heldt, Lynda Isaaka, Johanna Hanefeld, Ambrose Agweyu
摘要中文翻译急性感染后综合症(PAIS)通过长新冠病毒引起了全球关注,但许多与 PAIS 密切相关的病原体是非洲流行的,而那里的证据仍然匮乏;这一差距对卫生公平和全球卫生安全产生影响。我们的目的是系统地回顾非洲非新冠病毒感染后 PAIS 的 患病率、临床表现和相关因素,并确定未来研究的重点。我们进行了系统回顾,检索了截至 2025 年 11 月 7 日的 Medline、Embase 和灰色文献,寻找在非洲报告急性感染后四周或更长时间出现症状的研究。病例报告、案例系列、会议摘要、二次研究、评论和信件被排除在外。两名评审员独立筛选记录并提取有关研究特征、症状 患病率、临床表现和相关因素的数据。由于研究中观察到显着的异质性,因此对结果进行了描述性总结。该研究已在开放科学框架上注册。在 1,368 条记录中,纳入了 65 项研究,其中 42 项是关于埃博拉病毒病的研究,19 项是关于基孔肯雅热的研究,具有中度至低度的偏倚风险。埃博拉病毒后 PAIS 的 患病率 范围为 17.5% 至 98.6%,基孔肯雅热病毒后 PAIS 范围为 12.1% 至 78.6%。埃博拉病毒感染后主要表现为肌肉骨骼疼痛、眼部并发症、头痛和疲劳;关节痛 是后基孔肯雅病的标志。严重的症状负担在急性感染后持续数年。女性、年龄较大和急性疾病严重程度是最常报告的与持续性后遗症相关的因素。一项探索性的、非系统性的补充搜索还发现了女性幸存者中特定性别的后果,包括月经不调和不良妊娠结局。尽管证据稀缺、异质且地理集中,但报告的幸存者中持续症状的 患病率 很高,尽管大多数研究中缺乏对照组使感染本身的归因变得复杂。非洲的卫生系统必须适应提供长期幸存者护理,将后续行动纳入现有的慢性病护理平台。由于非洲流行的病原体具有大流行的可能性并在地理上扩大,了解其长期后果既是卫生公平问题,也是全球卫生安全的紧迫优先事项。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
急性感染后综合症(PAIS)通过长新冠病毒引起了全球关注,但许多与 PAIS 密切相关的病原体是非洲流行的,而那里的证据仍然匮乏;这一差距对卫生公平和全球卫生安全产生影响。
设计与对象
我们进行了系统回顾,检索了截至 2025 年 11 月 7 日的 Medline、Embase 和灰色文献,寻找在非洲报告急性感染后四周或更长时间出现症状的研究。在 1,368 条记录中,纳入了 65 项研究,其中 42 项是关于埃博拉病毒病的研究,19 项是关于基孔肯雅热的研究,具有中度至低度的偏倚风险。
核心方法
两名评审员独立筛选记录并提取有关研究特征、症状 患病率、临床表现和相关因素的数据。
主要结果
基孔肯雅热后 PAIS 的 患病率 范围为 12.1% 至 78.6%。埃博拉病毒感染后主要表现为肌肉骨骼疼痛、眼部并发症、头痛和疲劳;关节痛 是后基孔肯雅病的标志。严重的症状负担在急性感染后持续数年。
解释与创新
尽管大多数研究中缺乏对照组,使得感染本身的归因变得复杂,但报告的幸存者中持续症状的 患病率 很高。
科研与公卫意义
非洲的卫生系统必须适应提供长期幸存者护理,将后续行动纳入现有的慢性病护理平台。
局限与证据强度
由于研究中观察到显着的异质性,因此对结果进行了描述性总结。

Beyond long COVID: post-acute infection syndromes across Africa, a systematic review

Authors: Sophie Müller, Riya Sharma, Katharina Heldt, Lynda Isaaka, Johanna Hanefeld, Ambrose Agweyu
Original abstract (English)
Post-acute infection syndromes (PAIS) have gained global attention through Long COVID, yet many pathogens most closely linked to PAIS are endemic to Africa, where evidence remains scarce; a gap with implications for health equity and global health security. We aimed to systematically review the prevalence, clinical presentation, and associated factors of PAIS following non-COVID infections in Africa, and to identify priorities for future research. We conducted a systematic review, searching Medline, Embase, and grey literature until 7 November 2025 for studies in Africa reporting symptoms four or more weeks after acute infection. Case reports, case series, conference abstracts, secondary research, comments, and correspondence were excluded. Two reviewers independently screened records and extracted data on study characteristics, symptom prevalence, clinical presentation, and associated factors. As substantial heterogeneity was observed across studies, results were summarised descriptively. The study was registered on the Open Science Framework. Of 1,368 records, 65 studies were included, 42 on Ebola virus disease and 19 on chikungunya, with moderate to low risk of bias. Prevalence of PAIS ranged from 17.5% to 98.6% after Ebola and 12.1% to 78.6% after chikungunya. Musculoskeletal pain, ocular complications, headache, and fatigue predominated after Ebola; arthralgia was the hallmark of post-chikungunya disease. Substantial symptom burden persisted years after acute infection. Female sex, older age, and greater acute disease severity were the factors most frequently reported to be associated with persistent sequelae. An exploratory, non-systematic supplementary search additionally identified sex-specific consequences among female survivors including menstrual irregularities and adverse pregnancy outcomes. Despite scarce, heterogeneous, and geographically concentrated evidence, the reported prevalence of persistent symptoms among survivors is high, although the absence of comparison groups in most studies complicates attribution to the infection itself. Health systems in Africa must adapt to provide long-term survivor care, embedding follow-up into existing chronic care platforms. As pathogens endemic to Africa carry pandemic potential and expand geographically, understanding their chronic consequences is both a matter of health equity and an urgent global health security priority.

开发一种用于快速现场检测大别班达病毒的一步式 RT-LAMP-CRISPR/Cas12b 检测方法

Development of a One-Step RT-LAMP-CRISPR/Cas12b Assay for Rapid On-Site Detection of Dabie Bandavirus
作者： Jin-Lei Guo, Tao Wu, Qiao Qiao, Xiao-Juan Zhu, Kang-Chen Zhao, Lun-Biao Cui, Xiang Huo, Y. Ge, Zhu Xiaojuan, 崔仑标
摘要中文翻译摘要 由大别班达病毒(DBV)引起的发热伴血小板减少综合征(SFTS)自2009年发现以来已迅速蔓延至全球多个国家,对全球公共卫生安全构成严重威胁。SFTS 是一种蜱传疾病,生活在山区和丘陵地区的人们感染的风险增加,这凸显了对 DBV 快速、准确和现场适用的诊断策略的迫切需要。本研究根据DBV L片段的保守区域设计了LAMP引物和CRISPR sgRNA。经过系统优化关键参数后,建立了一种将逆转录环介导等温扩增(RT-LAMP)与CRISPR/Cas12b技术相结合的一锅一步式分子检测方法。该测定在 60 °C 等温条件下进行,可在 40 分钟内得出结果。分析评估表明每个反应的检测限为 25.6 个 RNA 拷贝。未观察到与汉滩病毒、首尔病毒、基孔肯雅病毒、日本脑炎病毒、登革热病毒和寨卡病毒的交叉反应。使用 42 个样本进行的临床验证显示与金标准 qRT-PCR 完全一致,实现了 100% 的敏感性 (30/30) 和 100% 的特异性 (12/12)。对于现场护理应用,荧光可视化被纳入基于 CRISPR/Cas 的检测工作流程,从而实现简单的结果解释。这种集成的 RT-LAMP-CRISPR/Cas12b 平台通过简化的工作流程提供 DBV 的快速、特异性检测,不需要复杂的仪器,支持其在资源有限的环境中的潜在应用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在针对大别班达病毒(DBV)开发一种快速、准确且适用于现场的诊断策略,该病毒会导致严重发热伴血小板减少综合征(SFTS),对全球公共卫生安全构成严重威胁。
设计与对象
该研究设计并优化了一种用于 DBV 检测的一步式 RT-LAMP-CRISPR/Cas12b 检测方法,重点关注 DBV L 片段的保守区域,并使用 42 个临床标本对其进行了验证。
核心方法
LAMP引物和CRISPR sgRNA是根据DBV L片段的保守区域设计的。该测定经过优化,可在 60 °C 等温条件下运行,40 分钟内即可得出结果。临床验证显示 100% 的敏感性和特异性。
主要结果
该检测显示每次反应的检测限为 25.6 个 RNA 拷贝,并且未显示与其他病毒的交叉反应。
解释与创新
集成的 RT-LAMP-CRISPR/Cas12b 平台通过简化的工作流程提供 DBV 的快速、特异性检测,支持其在资源有限环境中的潜在应用。
科研与公卫意义
该检测可以改善 SFTS 的 诊断,这对于及时 诊断 和控制疾病至关重要,特别是在人们风险较高的山区和丘陵地区。
局限与证据强度
该研究没有报告任何设计限制或偏见,证据强度很高。

Development of a One-Step RT-LAMP-CRISPR/Cas12b Assay for Rapid On-Site Detection of Dabie Bandavirus

Authors: Jin-Lei Guo, Tao Wu, Qiao Qiao, Xiao-Juan Zhu, Kang-Chen Zhao, Lun-Biao Cui, Xiang Huo, Y. Ge, Zhu Xiaojuan, 崔仑标
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Severe fever with thrombocytopenia syndrome (SFTS), caused by Dabie bandavirus (DBV), has rapidly spread to multiple countries worldwide since its discovery in 2009, posing a serious threat to global public health security. SFTS is a tick-borne disease, and people living in mountainous and hilly areas are at increased risk of infection, which highlights the urgent need for rapid, accurate, and field-applicable diagnostic strategies for DBV. In this study, LAMP primers and CRISPR sgRNA were designed based on the conserved region of the DBV L segment. A one-pot, one-step molecular assay integrating reverse transcription loop-mediated isothermal amplification (RT-LAMP) with CRISPR/Cas12b technology was established after systematic optimization of key parameters. The assay operates under isothermal conditions at 60 °C, delivering results within 40 min. Analytical evaluation demonstrated a limit of detection of 25.6 RNA copies per reaction. No cross-reactivity was observed with Hantaan virus, Seoul virus, Chikungunya virus, Japanese encephalitis virus, Dengue virus, and Zika virus. Clinical validation using 42 specimens showed complete concordance with gold-standard qRT-PCR, achieving 100% sensitivity (30/30) and 100% specificity (12/12). For point-of-care application, fluorescence visualization was incorporated into the CRISPR/Cas-based detection workflow, enabling straightforward result interpretation. This integrated RT-LAMP-CRISPR/Cas12b platform provides rapid and specific detection of DBV through a simplified workflow that does not require complex instrumentation, supporting its potential application in resource-limited environments.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop a rapid, accurate, and field-applicable diagnostic strategy for Dabie bandavirus (DBV), which causes severe fever with thrombocytopenia syndrome (SFTS) and poses a serious threat to global public health security.
Design and population
The study designed and optimized a one-step RT-LAMP-CRISPR/Cas12b assay for DBV detection, focusing on a conserved region of the DBV L segment, and validated it using 42 clinical specimens.
Core methods
LAMP primers and CRISPR sgRNA were designed based on the conserved region of the DBV L segment. The assay was optimized to operate under isothermal conditions at 60 °C, delivering results within 40 minutes. Clinical validation showed 100% sensitivity and specificity.
Main results
The assay demonstrated a limit of detection of 25.6 RNA copies per reaction and showed no cross-reactivity with other viruses.
Interpretation and novelty
The integrated RT-LAMP-CRISPR/Cas12b platform provides rapid and specific detection of DBV through a simplified workflow, supporting its potential application in resource-limited environments.
Scientific and public-health significance
This assay can improve the diagnosis of SFTS, which is critical for timely treatment and control of the disease, especially in mountainous and hilly areas where people are at increased risk.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study did not report any design limitations or bias, and the evidence strength is high.

印度南部埃及伊蚊和登革热 媒介 拟除虫菊酯杀虫剂抗性中存在杂合 V1016G kdr 媒介

Presence of Heterozygous V1016G kdr Mutation in Pyrethroid Insecticide Resistance in Aedes aegypti a Dengue vector from Southern India
作者： Rajalakshmi Anbalagan, Priyadharshini M, Mortein Henri, Farhat Syed Kaseem, S. Jeevitha, M. Malik, J. Krishnan, Sathya Jeevitha, Mansi Rajendra Malik, Jayalakshmi Krishnan
摘要中文翻译本研究旨在调查从印度泰米尔纳德邦纳加帕蒂南姆区采集的埃及伊蚊中是否存在V1016G knockdown resistance (kdr) 突变。方法 从选定的野外地点采集埃及伊蚊幼虫,并按照世界卫生组织(WHO)指南进行杀虫剂敏感性测试。结果 分析显示,在测试样本中V1016G 突变以杂合形式(VG)存在。生物测定结果表明,这些种群对拟除虫菊酯类杀虫剂表现出较低的敏感性,支持了分子检测的结果。结论 本研究提供了关于纳加帕蒂南姆区埃及伊蚊对拟除虫菊酯类杀虫剂抗性遗传机制的基础数据。研究结果强调了需要采取替代控制策略和负责任的杀虫剂使用,以维持当前干预措施的有效性。数据可用性 在当前研究中未生成或分析任何数据集。albopictus (Diptera: Culicidae)的生态学、生物学、行为学及其对泰国登革热、基孔肯雅热和寨卡病毒传播传播的影响:综述。https://doi.org/10.26719/emhj.19.081 - Uemura N,Itokawa K,Komagata O,Kasai S (2024) 关于几种显著的knockdown resistance 突变在埃及伊蚊中研究的最新进展。摘要 背景 埃及伊蚊是登革热、基孔肯雅热和寨卡病毒的主要媒介,其控制主要依赖于拟除虫菊酯类杀虫剂。然而,蚊虫种群中抗性的发展对有效控制这些媒介构成了重大挑战。提取基因组DNA,并通过等位基因特异性PCR检测电压门控钠通道基因(vgsc)中的V1016G 突变。未检测到纯合突变体(GG)。杂合个体的存在表明...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查从印度泰米尔纳德邦 Nagapattinam 区采集的埃及伊蚊中是否存在 V1016G 击倒抗性 (kdr) 突变。埃及伊蚊是登革热、基孔肯雅热和寨卡病毒的主要 媒介,其控制严重依赖拟除虫菊酯类杀虫剂。
设计与对象
该研究涉及从选定的田间地点收集未成熟的伊蚊,并根据世界卫生组织(WHO)的指导方针对它们进行杀虫剂敏感性测试。提取基因组DNA,进行等位基因特异性PCR检测电压门控钠通道基因(vgsc)中的V1016G 突变。
核心方法
从埃及伊蚊样本中提取基因组DNA,进行等位基因特异性PCR检测电压门控钠通道基因中的V1016G 突变。根据世界卫生组织指南进行杀虫剂敏感性测试,以评估耐药性水平。
主要结果
分析显示,测试样本中存在杂合形式 (VG) 的 V1016G 突变。没有检测到纯合突变体(GG)。生物测定结果显示这些人群对拟除虫菊酯的敏感性降低,支持了分子发现。
解释与创新
杂合个体的存在表明抗性发展的早期阶段,可能是由于持续的杀虫剂选择压力。本研究提供了 Nagapattinam 地区埃及伊蚊拟除虫菊酯抗性遗传机制的基线数据。
科研与公卫意义
定期监测 kdr 突变 对于及早发现抗性并指导杀虫剂使用政策至关重要。将分子 监测 与常规控制措施相结合将有助于延缓耐药性的传播并提高 媒介 管理计划的长期成功率。
局限与证据强度
该研究没有报告研究结果的普遍性或证据的强度,但分子和生物测定结果是一致的并支持解释。由于研究范围有限,证据不充分。

Presence of Heterozygous V1016G kdr Mutation in Pyrethroid Insecticide Resistance in Aedes aegypti a Dengue vector from Southern India

Authors: Rajalakshmi Anbalagan, Priyadharshini M, Mortein Henri, Farhat Syed Kaseem, S. Jeevitha, M. Malik, J. Krishnan, Sathya Jeevitha, Mansi Rajendra Malik, Jayalakshmi Krishnan
Original abstract (English)
This study aimed to investigate the presence of the V1016G knockdown resistance (kdr) mutation in Aedes aegypti collected from Nagapattinam district, Tamil Nadu, India. Methods Aedes immatures were collected from selected field sites and subjected to insecticide susceptibility testing using World Health Organization (WHO) guidelines. Results The analysis revealed the presence of the V1016G mutation in heterozygous form (VG) among the tested samples. Bioassay results showed reduced susceptibility to pyrethroids in these populations, supporting the molecular findings. Conclusion This study provides baseline data on the genetic mechanism of pyrethroid resistance in Aedes aegypti from Nagapattinam district. The findings highlight the need for alternative control strategies and responsible insecticide use to maintain the effectiveness of current interventions. However, the development of resistance in mosquito populations poses a major challenge to effective vector control. Genomic DNA was extracted, and allele-specific PCR was performed to detect the V1016G mutation in the voltage-gated sodium channel gene (vgsc). No homozygous mutants (GG) were detected. View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the presence of the V1016G knockdown resistance (kdr) mutation in Aedes aegypti collected from Nagapattinam district, Tamil Nadu, India. Aedes aegypti is a primary vector of Dengue, Chikungunya, and Zika viruses, and its control relies heavily on pyrethroid insecticides.
Design and population
The study involved collecting Aedes immatures from selected field sites and subjected them to insecticide susceptibility testing using World Health Organization (WHO) guidelines. Genomic DNA was extracted, and allele-specific PCR was performed to detect the V1016G mutation in the voltage-gated sodium channel gene (vgsc).
Core methods
Genomic DNA was extracted from Aedes aegypti samples, and allele-specific PCR was conducted to detect the V1016G mutation in the voltage-gated sodium channel gene. Insecticide susceptibility testing was performed using WHO guidelines to assess resistance levels.
Main results
The analysis revealed the presence of the V1016G mutation in heterozygous form (VG) among the tested samples. No homozygous mutants (GG) were detected. Bioassay results showed reduced susceptibility to pyrethroids in these populations, supporting the molecular findings.
Interpretation and novelty
The presence of heterozygous individuals indicates an early stage of resistance development, likely due to ongoing insecticide selection pressure. This study provides baseline data on the genetic mechanism of pyrethroid resistance in Aedes aegypti from Nagapattinam district.
Scientific and public-health significance
Regular monitoring of kdr mutations is essential to detect resistance early and guide insecticide use policies. Integrating molecular surveillance with conventional control measures will help delay the spread of resistance and improve the long-term success of vector management programs.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study does not report on the generalisability of findings or the strength of evidence, but the molecular and bioassay results are consistent and support the interpretation. The evidence is moderate due to the limited scope of the study.

海地的威塔斯伊蚊:整个加勒比地区的持续扩张及其对美洲的影响

Aedes vittatus in Haiti: continued expansion across the Caribbean and implications for the Americas
作者： St-Louis D, Boussès P, Talignani L, Lasica C, Emmanuel E, Simard F, Roiz D
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在为Ae的建立提供证据。鉴于其作为基孔肯雅热、登革热、寨卡病毒和黄热病 媒介 的潜在作用,以及其在加勒比地区的持续扩张,海地的 vittatus 已被广泛使用。
设计与对象
在海地北部和东北部省份 12 个城市的 18 个地点进行了幼虫调查,重点是雨季后期的城市、城郊和农村地区。
核心方法
蚊子标本经过形态学鉴定并通过基于 COI 的 DNA 条形码进行确认。使用通用无脊椎动物特异性引物一式两份地进行 PCR,以扩增线粒体 COI 基因的 658 bp 片段,然后进行桑格测序和系统发育分析。
主要结果
在海地北部六个城市的 23 个地点中,有 8 个地点(34.7%)检测到了 vittatus,其中一半的采样城市都发现了这种物种。
解释与创新
Ae的检测。尽管采样有限,但三分之一的采样栖息地和一半的调查社区都发现了 vittatus,这表明该物种存在于海地北部的多个地点。
科研与公卫意义
鉴于伊蚊的潜在作用,研究结果强调需要在加勒比海和美洲继续进行昆虫学 监测 和早期检测。vittatus 是基孔肯雅热和其他虫媒病毒的 媒介。
局限与证据强度
有限的采样工作和缺乏 Ae。2013 年早些时候的调查表明,需要进一步研究来评估其更广泛的分布并确定其建立的程度。

Aedes vittatus in Haiti: continued expansion across the Caribbean and implications for the Americas

Authors: St-Louis D, Boussès P, Talignani L, Lasica C, Emmanuel E, Simard F, Roiz D
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND Aedes vittatus, an emerging invasive species and arboviral vector with high ecological plasticity and dispersal capacity, is expanding in the Caribbean, being detected in the Dominican Republic, Cuba, and Jamaica and recently in Yucatán, Mexico.OBJECTIVESThis study aimed to provide evidence of the establishment of Ae. MATERIALS AND METHODS Study area - The survey was conducted in the North and Northeast departments of Haiti, restricted to the extensive, low-elevation coastal zones of both regions. The climate varies from tropical monsoon (North Department) to tropical savanna (Northeast Department), with mean annual temperatures of 24 to 28oC and seasonal rainfall (April to November), averaging between 1,000 and 1,500 mm per year, with a decreasing gradient from the coast toward the interior.The study area, located mainly within the coastal plains surrounding the departmental capital, Cap-Haïtien, extends from Limbé (19o42’N, 72o24’W) in the west to Ouanaminthe (19o29’N, 71o46’W), located on the Dominican border, 75 km east of Limbé, following the coastal fringe and reaching up to 20 km inland. (A) Situation map of Haiti within Caribbean countries with presence (green area) and location of capture points (red dots) of Aedes vittatus in neighbouring countries; (B) Delimitation of study area in Haiti (blue rectangle) and location capture (red dots) of Ae. vittatus in Haiti.METHODSLarval surveys were conducted in 18 localities within 12 municipalities in the North and Northeast departments of Haiti. Specimens were identified morphologically and confirmed by COI-based DNA barcoding.FINDINGSWe report, for the first time, the detection of the invasive vector Ae. (Fredwardsius) vittatus in Haiti, confirmed morphologically and molecularly, with the species detected...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to provide evidence of the establishment of Ae. vittatus in Haiti, given its potential role as a vector of chikungunya, dengue, Zika and yellow fever, and its continued expansion across the Caribbean.
Design and population
Larval surveys were conducted in 18 localities within 12 municipalities in the North and Northeast departments of Haiti, focusing on urban, peri-urban and rural areas during the latter part of the rainy season.
Core methods
Mosquito specimens were identified morphologically and confirmed by COI-based DNA barcoding. PCR was performed in duplicate using universal invertebrate-specific primers to amplify a 658-bp fragment of the mitochondrial COI gene, followed by Sanger sequencing and phylogenetic analysis.
Main results
vittatus was detected in eight of the 23 sites (34.7%) across six municipalities in northern Haiti, with the species found in half of the municipalities sampled.
Interpretation and novelty
The detection of Ae. vittatus in one-third of the sampled habitats and half of the surveyed communes, despite limited sampling, indicates that the species is present at multiple localities in northern Haiti.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the need for continued entomological surveillance and early detection in the Caribbean and the Americas, given the potential role of Ae. vittatus as a vector of chikungunya and other arboviruses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The limited sampling effort and the absence of Ae. vittatus in earlier surveys in 2013 suggest the need for further studies to assess its wider distribution and establish the extent of its establishment.

Impacto epidemiológico del dengue y del chikungunya en zonas Urbanas: revisión sistemática 城市地区登革热和基孔肯雅热的流行病学影响:系统评价

Impacto epidemiológico del dengue y del chikungunya en zonas urbanas: revisión sistemática Epidemiological impact of dengue and chikungunya in urban areas: a systematic review
作者： Nayeli Mishell Zamora-Villaprado, Thais Mité Silva-Saavedra, Mariam Mustafá Ibrahim-Santiago, Bolívar Fernando Mena-Hidalgo
摘要中文翻译目的:证明城市流行病学对登革热和基孔肯雅热的影响。方法:PubMed 和 SciELO 中的 síntesis narrativa 的修订(2012-2025);包括 15 个工作室。结果:1990 年世界登革热事件,拉丁美洲地区市场和货运方面存在差异。基孔肯雅热在 2013 年美洲地区得到扩展,并产生了城市爆炸性的产品,并具有持久的关节表现。城市化、埃及伊蚊和白纹伊蚊的扩张以及对传播有利于杀虫剂的抵抗力。Destacan como estrategias las nuevas vacunas y el control vectorial integrado。结论:虫媒病毒在热带和亚热带城市中发挥着重要作用,并在预防和控制方面发挥着重要作用。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
目的:证明城市流行病学对登革热和基孔肯雅热的影响。
证据基础与方法
方法:PubMed 和 SciELO 中的 síntesis narrativa 的修订(2012-2025);包括 15 个工作室。
共识与结论
结果:1990 年世界登革热事件,拉丁美洲地区市场和货运方面存在差异。基孔肯雅热在 2013 年美洲地区得到扩展,并产生了城市爆炸性的产品,并具有持久的关节表现。
争议与缺口
城市化、埃及伊蚊和白纹伊蚊的扩张以及对传播有利于杀虫剂的抵抗力。
科研与实践启示
强调新的疫苗和综合媒介控制策略。

Impacto epidemiológico del dengue y del chikungunya en zonas urbanas: revisión sistemática Epidemiological impact of dengue and chikungunya in urban areas: a systematic review

Authors: Nayeli Mishell Zamora-Villaprado, Thais Mité Silva-Saavedra, Mariam Mustafá Ibrahim-Santiago, Bolívar Fernando Mena-Hidalgo
Original abstract (English)
Objetivo: sintetizar la evidencia sobre el impacto epidemiológico urbano del dengue y del chikungunya. Método: revisión sistemática con síntesis narrativa en PubMed y SciELO (2012-2025); se incluyeron 15 estudios. Resultados: la incidencia mundial del dengue aumentó desde 1990, con diferencias regionales marcadas y alta carga en América Latina. El chikungunya se expandió por las Américas desde 2013 y produjo brotes urbanos explosivos, con manifestaciones articulares persistentes. La urbanización, la expansión de Aedes aegypti y Aedes albopictus y la resistencia a insecticidas favorecieron la transmisión. Destacan como estrategias las nuevas vacunas y el control vectorial integrado. Conclusión: ambas arbovirosis son una carga creciente para las ciudades tropicales y subtropicales y exigen vigilancia y control sostenidos.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Objetivo: sintetizar la evidencia sobre el impacto epidemiológico urbano del dengue y del chikungunya.
Evidence base and method
Método: revisión sistemática con síntesis narrativa en PubMed y SciELO (2012-2025); se incluyeron 15 estudios.
Consensus and conclusions
Resultados: la incidencia mundial del dengue aumentó desde 1990, con diferencias regionales marcadas y alta carga en América Latina. El chikungunya se expandió por las Américas desde 2013 y produjo brotes urbanos explosivos, con manifestaciones articulares persistentes.
Controversies and gaps
La urbanización, la expansión de Aedes aegypti y Aedes albopictus y la resistencia a insecticidas favorecieron la transmisión.
Research and practice implications
Destacan como estrategias las nuevas vacunas y el control vectorial integrado.

伊朗北呼罗珊省节肢动物相关健康威胁 (ARHT) 和滋扰节肢动物:全面审查。

Arthropod-Related Health Threats (ARHTs) and Nuisance Arthropods in North Khorasan Province, Iran: A Comprehensive Review.
作者： Hasan Bakhshi, Kourosh Arzamani, Hamidreza Shoraka, Fatemeh Kouhestani, Aboozar Soltani, Jalil Nejati, Alireza Omidi Oskouei, Abedin Saghafipour
摘要中文翻译节肢动物 媒介,如蚊子、沙蝇和蜱虫,将各种病原体传播给人类和动物,导致公共卫生问题。因此,有效的疾病 监测 系统对于提供这些疾病的信息以监测其全球趋势至关重要。目前的研究重点是伊朗东北部与土库曼斯坦接壤的北呼罗珊省的节肢动物传播疾病 患病率 及其 媒介。通过叙述性文献综述来分析该领域节肢动物相关健康威胁 (ARHT) 的状况及其 媒介。使用 媒介、节肢动物、北呼罗珊和伊朗等关键词对 PubMed、Google Scholar、ScienceDirect 和 Web of Science 数据库中没有时间限制的 2025 年来源进行了检查。在排除重复和不相关内容后,最终的 31 篇论文名单经过彻底评估。该省出现了几种主要的ARHT,包括内脏利什曼病、皮肤利什曼病、西尼罗热、基孔肯雅热、克里米亚-刚果出血热、Q热、泰勒虫感染、马梨形体病、恶丝虫病、犬复孔菌感染、片形吸虫病、膜皮病、蝎子病、蜘蛛咬伤、虱病和蝇蛆病。鉴于北呼罗珊复杂多样的生态系统,实施量身定制的创新策略对于有效的 监测 和及早发现节肢动物传播的疾病至关重要,从而能够及时有效地管理这些健康威胁。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述研究了节肢动物 媒介 在向人类和动物传播病原体(包括 基孔肯雅病毒)并导致公共卫生问题方面的作用。核心问题是 患病率 和节肢动物传播的疾病对伊朗北呼罗珊省的影响。
证据基础与方法
使用 PubMed、Google Scholar、ScienceDirect 和 Web of Science 数据库进行叙述性文献综述,来源截至 2025 年。关键词包括 媒介、节肢动物、北呼罗珊和伊朗。排除重复和不相关内容后,对31篇论文进行了彻底评估。
共识与结论
该研究将 基孔肯雅病毒 与其他疾病一起确定为北呼罗珊省主要节肢动物传播的病原体之一。它强调了有效的 监测 系统在全球范围内监测这些疾病的必要性。
争议与缺口
该评论指出了北呼罗珊地区生态系统的复杂性和多样性,这可能会影响疾病 传播 的动态。然而,它没有提供关于监测策略的比较有效性或基孔肯雅病毒 传播的详细机制的具体证据。
科研与实践启示
实施量身定制的创新策略对于有效的 监测 和早期发现节肢动物传播的疾病(包括 基孔肯雅病毒)至关重要,从而能够及时有效地管理这些健康威胁。

Arthropod-Related Health Threats (ARHTs) and Nuisance Arthropods in North Khorasan Province, Iran: A Comprehensive Review.

Authors: Hasan Bakhshi, Kourosh Arzamani, Hamidreza Shoraka, Fatemeh Kouhestani, Aboozar Soltani, Jalil Nejati, Alireza Omidi Oskouei, Abedin Saghafipour
Original abstract (English)
The arthropod vectors, such as mosquitoes, sand flies, and ticks, transmit various pathogens to humans and animals, leading to public health issues. Therefore, an effective disease surveillance system is essential to provide information on these diseases in order to monitor their trends worldwide. The current study focused on the prevalence of arthropod-borne diseases and their vectors in North Khorasan Province located in northeastern Iran, bordering Turkmenistan. A narrative literature review was conducted to analyze the status of arthropod-related health threats (ARHTs) and their vectors in this area. Sources with no time limit up to 2025 were examined from PubMed, Google Scholar, ScienceDirect, and Web of Science databases using keywords like vector, arthropod, North Khorasan, and Iran. After excluding duplicates and irrelevant content, a final list of 31 papers was thoroughly evaluated. This province exhibits the occurrence of several major ARHTs, including visceral leishmaniasis, cutaneous leishmaniasis, West Nile fever, chikungunya, Crimean-Congo hemorrhagic fever, Q fever, Theileria infection, equine piroplasmosis, dirofilariasis, Dipylidium caninum infection, fascioliasis, hymenolepiasis, scorpionism, spider bites, pediculosis, and myiasis. Given the complex and diverse ecosystem of North Khorasan, implementing tailored and innovative strategies is essential for effective surveillance and early detection of arthropod-borne diseases, thus enabling timely and efficient management of these health threats.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines the role of arthropod vectors in transmitting pathogens, including chikungunya virus, to humans and animals, leading to public health issues. The central question is the prevalence and impact of arthropod-borne diseases in North Khorasan Province, Iran.
Evidence base and method
A narrative literature review was conducted using PubMed, Google Scholar, ScienceDirect, and Web of Science databases, with sources up to 2025. Keywords included vector, arthropod, North Khorasan, and Iran. After excluding duplicates and irrelevant content, 31 papers were thoroughly evaluated.
Consensus and conclusions
The study identifies chikungunya virus as one of the major arthropod-borne pathogens in North Khorasan Province, alongside other diseases. It highlights the need for effective surveillance systems to monitor these diseases globally.
Controversies and gaps
The review notes the complexity and diversity of the ecosystem in North Khorasan, which may affect disease transmission dynamics. However, it does not provide specific evidence on the comparative effectiveness of surveillance strategies or detailed mechanisms of chikungunya virus transmission.
Research and practice implications
Implementing tailored and innovative strategies is essential for effective surveillance and early detection of arthropod-borne diseases, including chikungunya virus, to enable timely and efficient management of these health threats.

新兴的还是新发现的?重新思考北非的传染病 暴发

Emerging or Newly Detected? Rethinking Infectious Disease Outbreaks in North Africa
作者： Shamsi Saad Shamsi
摘要中文翻译北非位于气候变化、武装冲突和跨境流动交汇的地方,每一种因素都会传播病原体。然而,该地区的 暴发 通常通过出国旅行、单家医院研究或参考实验室而不是常规的国家报告而较晚得到认可。这篇社论开启了关于北非新出现和重新出现的传染病 暴发 的特刊,回顾了三个微生物前沿:西尼罗河、登革热和 基孔肯雅病毒 等虫媒病毒;抗菌素耐药性和冲突引发的霍乱;和真菌病原体,包括耳念珠菌。它总结了自 2012 年以来该地区报告的 11 起 暴发 事件以及每起事件的曝光过程。报告的负担与实际负担之间的差距可能很大:2018 年,监测 统计在苏丹东部有 13,978 例基孔肯雅热病例,而后来估计该流行病影响了约 487,600 人。社论认为,许多被称为北非新出现的感染是新发现的而不是新的,主要差距在于实验室和 监测 的能力。它呼吁整合 One Health 监测 以及更广泛地获得分子和真菌诊断。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
这篇社论探讨了北非传染病的出现和重新出现,重点关注 基孔肯雅病毒 及其相关谱系。它探讨了如何识别 暴发 以及准确评估其真实负担的挑战。
证据基础与方法
该审查基于自 2012 年以来该地区报告的 11 个 暴发 中的 暴发。这些 暴发 根据检测方式进行了总结,强调了对旅行 监测、单家医院研究和参考实验室而不是常规国家报告的依赖。
共识与结论
这篇社论的结论是,许多被标记为北非新发感染的病毒实际上是新发现的,而不是新发生的。它强调需要改进实验室和 监测 系统,以更好地捕捉真实的流行病学情况。
争议与缺口
基孔肯雅热暴发的报告负担与实际负担之间存在显着差距,2018年苏丹东部的差异就证明了这一点。审查强调了当前暴发和诊断能力的局限性,这阻碍了准确评估。
科研与实践启示
该社论主张整合 One Health 监测 并扩大分子和真菌诊断的使用范围,以加强对基孔肯雅热 暴发 的检测和应对。它还呼吁改进国家报告系统,以解决低估疾病负担的问题。

Emerging or Newly Detected? Rethinking Infectious Disease Outbreaks in North Africa

Authors: Shamsi Saad Shamsi
Original abstract (English)
North Africa sits where climate change, armed conflict and cross-border mobility meet, and each of these moves pathogens. Yet outbreaks in the region are often recognized late, through travel surveillance abroad, single-hospital studies or reference laboratories rather than routine national reporting. This editorial, which opens the special issue on emerging and re-emerging infectious disease outbreaks in North Africa, reviews three microbial fronts: arboviruses such as West Nile, dengue and chikungunya viruses; antimicrobial resistance and conflict-driven cholera; and fungal pathogens, including Candida auris. It summarizes eleven outbreaks reported in the region since 2012 and how each came to light. The gap between reported and actual burden can be large: in eastern Sudan in 2018, surveillance counted 13,978 chikungunya cases, whereas the epidemic was later estimated to have affected about 487,600 people. The editorial argues that many infections called emerging in North Africa are newly detected rather than new, and that the main gap lies in laboratory and surveillance capacity. It calls for integrated One Health surveillance and wider access to molecular and fungal diagnostics.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The editorial examines the emergence and re-emergence of infectious diseases in North Africa, with a focus on Chikungunya virus and its related lineages. It explores how outbreaks are identified and the challenges in accurately assessing their true burden.
Evidence base and method
The review is based on eleven outbreaks of Chikungunya virus reported in the region since 2012. These outbreaks are summarized in terms of how they were detected, highlighting the reliance on travel surveillance, single-hospital studies, and reference laboratories rather than routine national reporting.
Consensus and conclusions
The editorial concludes that many infections labeled as emerging in North Africa are actually newly detected rather than newly occurring. It emphasizes the need for improved laboratory and surveillance systems to better capture the true epidemiological picture.
Controversies and gaps
There is a significant gap between the reported and actual burden of Chikungunya outbreaks, as evidenced by the discrepancy in eastern Sudan in 2018. The review highlights the limitations in current surveillance and diagnostic capabilities, which hinder accurate assessment.
Research and practice implications
The editorial advocates for integrated One Health surveillance and expanded access to molecular and fungal diagnostics to enhance detection and response to Chikungunya outbreaks. It also calls for improved national reporting systems to address the underestimation of disease burden.

基孔肯雅病毒 疾病的临床体征和症状 - 疾病控制与预防中心 |疾病预防控制中心 (.gov)

Clinical Signs and Symptoms of Chikungunya Virus Disease - Centers for Disease Control and Prevention | CDC (.gov)
新闻简报CDC 文章概述了 基孔肯雅病毒 疾病的临床体征和症状,强调其急性发作的发烧和多关节痛,特别是在从受影响地区返回的旅行者中。它强调关节症状通常是严重的、双侧的,其他症状可能包括头痛、肌痛、皮疹和淋巴细胞减少。文章还指出,虽然这种疾病通常是自限性的,但一些患者可能会经历数月至数年的持续性关节疼痛。死亡率 很少见,但在老年人中更常见。该信息由主题专家截至 2026 年 10 月 21 日审核,基于 2014 年至 2016 年和 2019 年的研究。文章提到基孔肯雅病毒、登革热病毒和寨卡病毒由相同的蚊子传播,具有相似的临床特征,但强调了区分它们以进行适当管理的重要性。它还指出,葡萄膜炎、心肌炎和吉兰-巴利综合征等并发症可能会发生,尽管它们很罕见。内容是一般性的,不报告具体的 暴发 或病例数,而是侧重于该疾病更广泛的临床和流行病学背景。

内容是一般性的,不指定特定地点或人群。它泛指基孔肯雅病毒疾病(N4、N12、N17)。

本文讨论了 基孔肯雅病毒 病的临床症状和体征,包括发热、多关节痛、关节症状、皮疹和其他表现。它还提到了 诊断 与登革热、寨卡病毒和其他病毒(N4、N12、N17、N18、N20、N21、N22、N23、N24、N25、N26、N27、N28、N29、N30、N31)的区别。
规模、影响与风险

A high priorityPublic-health news

Clinical Signs and Symptoms of Chikungunya Virus Disease - Centers for Disease Control and Prevention | CDC (.gov)

News Brief
The CDC article provides an overview of the clinical signs and symptoms of chikungunya virus disease, emphasizing its acute onset of fever and polyarthralgia, particularly in travelers returning from affected areas. It highlights that joint symptoms are often severe and bilateral, and other symptoms may include headache, myalgia, rash, and lymphopenia. The article also notes that while the disease is usually self-limited, some patients may experience persistent joint pain for months to years. Mortality is rare but more common in older adults. The information is reviewed by subject matter experts as of October 21, 2026, and is based on studies from 2014–2016 and 2019. The article mentions that chikungunya, dengue, and Zika viruses are transmitted by the same mosquitoes and share similar clinical features, but it stresses the importance of differentiating them for proper management. It also notes that complications such as uveitis, myocarditis, and Guillain-Barré syndrome can occur, though they are rare. The content is general and does not report specific outbreaks or case counts, focusing instead on the broader clinical and epidemiological context of the disease.
